# Milestone M2: request core and customer API

Ids follow registry section 3. Splits (lettered ids) are listed under "Registry deviations". Lane L2-core (stage S1, serial): M2.1a, M2.1, M2.2, M2.3a, M2.3, M2.4, M2.5, M2.6a; this whole set (M2.1a to M2.6a) is the "M2-core" merge unit that M3, M4 and M5 wait for (M2.6a carries `customer/waste-request.mapper.ts`, which M3.3 imports). Lane L2-cust (stage S2, serial, starts after the M2-core merge): M2.6b, M2.6c, M2.7, M2.8a, M2.8b, M2.9, then M2.10 (M2.10 depends only on M1.2 and touches no file of the other M2 tasks).

Read before any M2 task: registry sections 4.2 to 4.7, 4.10, 4.12, 4.13, 4.14, 5 (rows of `customer/waste-customer.controller.ts`) and 6; contract.md (plan root) section 7 for DTO fields. Spec = `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-17-recycle-rewards-design.md` (cited as "spec x.y"). Folder shorthand used only in prose: `wx/` = `apps/api/src/modules/waste-exchange/`. Every Files line uses the full path.

## Commands used by several M2 tasks

Commands are cited by their registry 4.14 block names and are not restated here: "registry 4.14 Node environment" (export it in every new shell), "registry 4.14 DB gate A", "registry 4.14 Lint" (for this part only the API line, `cd apps/api && npx eslint src --max-warnings=0`, exit 0; never the package `lint` script, which does not enforce zero warnings), the registry 4.14 race-spec command and "registry 4.14 Lane databases" (L2-core and L2-cust run their DB gates against the race container and port named in the worker's context line, substituted into every DB gate command and into `DATABASE_URL`; never `tubu_it_main` / 5550 while lanes run in parallel). Run from the worktree root unless a `cd` is shown.

- Unit spec: `cd apps/api && npx jest <path>`; pass = last summary line `Tests:       N passed, N total` with no `failed`.
- "Typecheck and lint exit 0" in a Gate or step means `pnpm --filter @tubutree/api typecheck` (exit 0, no `error TS` line) and `cd apps/api && npx eslint src --max-warnings=0` (registry 4.14 Lint, exit 0).
- Before the first `pnpm --filter @tubutree/api typecheck` in a fresh worktree (and again after any change under `packages/shared-types/src`), run "registry 4.14 Workspace build": `pnpm --filter @tubutree/shared-types build` (exit 0; `packages/shared-types/dist/waste.d.ts` exists). The api typecheck resolves `@tubutree/shared-types` through `dist`; jest maps it to `src`, so a missing build shows only at the typecheck (TS2307).
- Race spec: registry 4.14 DB gate A (expected last deploy line `All migrations have been successfully applied.`), then `cd apps/api && npx jest -c test/integration-race/jest.config.js --runInBand <name pattern>`; pass = `Tests:       N passed, N total` and no `40P01` or `P2034` text. Race specs are not in CI and `tsc` does not check them (`apps/api/tsconfig.json` excludes `test`; ts-jest still compiles each file, so a type error fails the run), so every task that adds SQL, a lock or a transaction runs its race command itself. Never touch the containers `tubu_pg`, `tubu_redis`, `chodeli-temp-db-12565`; never `pkill`; never stop a container you did not start.

Forced interleaving in race specs (registry 6: every pair in both orders, named `<A> then <B>` and `<B> then <A>`; registry 4.10 forced interleaving): import `raceWithHold` (and, where a case needs them, `holdLock`, `lockWaiters`, `waitUntil`, `blockedBackends`, `holdFirstCall`) from `./fixtures/waste-fixtures` and declare none of these names locally (a name both imported and declared fails TS2440 under ts-jest). `raceWithHold(prisma, lockSql, first, second)` holds `lockSql` on a side transaction, starts `first`, waits until it queues on the lock, starts `second`, waits until it queues too, releases, and returns both settled results in argument order. Use it only when both contenders take that lock before any replay or validation short-circuit. Never a sleep. Every race case asserts `expect(errorsOf(rs).join()).not.toMatch(/40P01|P2034/)`.

Config rows in race specs (registry 4.10 config cache): a spec that writes a `waste.*` row with `setConfig` after the module compiled then runs `(moduleRef.get(SystemConfigService) as unknown as { cache: Map<string, unknown> }).cache.clear();`, restores the saved rows in `afterAll` (rows that did not exist are deleted) and clears again. Never `SystemConfigService.set` in a test.

Customer race-spec scaffolding. `waste-create.race-spec.ts` (M2.8a), `waste-edit.race-spec.ts` (M2.8b) and `waste-tracking.race-spec.ts` (M2.9) each start with this block, copied locally (race specs import only `helpers.ts`, the waste fixtures and source modules). The `jest.mock` keeps `computeWastePublicFlags` from reading the real Gomdon switch, so the PICKUP lane is open when `waste.enabled` and `waste.lane_pickup_enabled` are true:

```ts
jest.mock('../../src/modules/integrations/gomdon/gomdon-config', () => ({
  ...jest.requireActual('../../src/modules/integrations/gomdon/gomdon-config'),
  isGomdonRecyclingEnabled: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(true),
}));

import { randomInt } from 'node:crypto';
import { Test, type TestingModule } from '@nestjs/testing';
import { Prisma } from '@prisma/client';
import type { WasteLineInput, WasteMethod } from '@tubutree/shared-types';
import { PrismaModule } from '../../src/prisma/prisma.module';
import { PrismaService } from '../../src/prisma/prisma.service';
import { SystemConfigService } from '../../src/modules/system-config/system-config.service';
import { WasteConfigService } from '../../src/modules/waste-exchange/core/waste-config.service';
import { WasteAccessService } from '../../src/modules/waste-exchange/core/waste-access.service';
import { WasteHoldService } from '../../src/modules/waste-exchange/core/waste-hold.service';
import { WasteRequestsService } from '../../src/modules/waste-exchange/customer/waste-requests.service';
import { createUser as createItUser, deleteItData, randCode, setConfig, warmPool } from './helpers';
import { cleanupWaste, createWarehouse, createWasteItem, createWastePoint, createWasteRequest, raceWithHold } from './fixtures/waste-fixtures';

const CONFIG_KEYS = ['waste.enabled', 'waste.lane_pickup_enabled'];
let moduleRef: TestingModule;
let prisma: PrismaService;
let svc: WasteRequestsService;
let savedConfig: { key: string; value: unknown }[] = [];
const userIds: string[] = [];
const pointIds: string[] = [];
const itemIds: string[] = [];
const storefrontIds: string[] = [];

const phone = () => `09${String(randomInt(0, 100_000_000)).padStart(8, '0')}`;
const randKey = () => `it-${randCode(12)}`;
const clearConfigCache = () => (moduleRef.get(SystemConfigService) as unknown as { cache: Map<string, unknown> }).cache.clear();
async function createUser(data: Parameters<typeof createItUser>[1] = {}) {
  const u = await createItUser(prisma, data);
  userIds.push(u.id);
  return u;
}
const customer = () => createUser({ phone: phone() });
const customerLock = (u: { id: string }) => Prisma.sql`SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(731001, hashtext(${u.id}))`;
const trackingLock = Prisma.sql`SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(731002, 0)`;
const errorsOf = (rs: PromiseSettledResult<unknown>[]) =>
  rs.flatMap((r) => (r.status === 'rejected' ? [String((r.reason as Error)?.message ?? r.reason)] : []));
const reasonOf = (r: PromiseSettledResult<unknown>) => String(((r as PromiseRejectedResult).reason as Error)?.message);
async function scene(itemOver: Parameters<typeof createWasteItem>[1] = {}) {
  const wh = await createWarehouse(prisma);
  const u = await customer();
  const owner = await customer();
  const item = await createWasteItem(prisma, itemOver);
  itemIds.push(item.id);
  const point = await createWastePoint(prisma, { kind: 'PARTNER', ownerUserId: owner.id, itemIds: [item.id] });
  pointIds.push(point.id);
  if (point.createdStorefront && point.ownerStorefrontId) storefrontIds.push(point.ownerStorefrontId);
  return { u, owner, item, point, wh };
}
const create = (u: { id: string }, key: string, lines: WasteLineInput[], method: WasteMethod = 'DROP_OFF', pointId?: string) =>
  svc.create(u.id, key, { method, lines, pointId, consent: true, ...(method === 'MAIL_IN' ? { packagingConsent: true, postagePaidConsent: true } : {}) });

beforeAll(async () => {
  moduleRef = await Test.createTestingModule({
    imports: [PrismaModule],
    providers: [SystemConfigService, WasteConfigService, WasteAccessService, WasteHoldService, WasteRequestsService],
  }).compile();
  prisma = moduleRef.get(PrismaService);
  svc = moduleRef.get(WasteRequestsService);
  await warmPool(prisma);
  savedConfig = await prisma.systemConfig.findMany({ where: { key: { in: CONFIG_KEYS } } });
  await setConfig(prisma, 'waste.enabled', true);
  await setConfig(prisma, 'waste.lane_pickup_enabled', true);
  clearConfigCache();
});

afterAll(async () => {
  await prisma.systemConfig.deleteMany({ where: { key: { in: CONFIG_KEYS.filter((k) => !savedConfig.some((c) => c.key === k)) } } });
  for (const c of savedConfig) await setConfig(prisma, c.key, c.value as never);
  clearConfigCache();
  await cleanupWaste(prisma, { pointIds, itemIds, storefrontIds });
  await deleteItData(prisma, { userIds });
  await moduleRef.close();
});
```

A task that needs more from this block (another import, provider or helper) names the addition in its own step. `createWasteRequest` and `trackingLock` are unused in `waste-create.race-spec.ts`; that is allowed (no `noUnused*` flag in `apps/api/tsconfig.json`, and ESLint does not lint `test/`).

Module wiring rule for M2: `WasteCoreModule` (`apps/api/src/modules/waste-exchange/core/waste-core.module.ts`) gains `providers` and `exports` in M2.1a, M2.2, M2.3, M2.4, M2.5 (one line each, appended); `WasteCustomerModule` gains `imports: [WasteCoreModule]`, providers and the controller in M2.6a to M2.7. `PrismaService`, `SystemConfigService`, `NotificationsService` and `LoyaltyService` come from global modules (`@Global()` in `prisma.module.ts`, `system-config.module.ts`, `notifications.module.ts`, `loyalty.module.ts`), so no other import is needed. Final state after M2.5 (M3, M4 and M5 rely on it): `@Module({ providers: [WasteConfigService, WasteEventsService, WasteNotifierService, WasteAccessService, WasteHoldService, WasteShipLockService], exports: [WasteConfigService, WasteEventsService, WasteNotifierService, WasteAccessService, WasteHoldService, WasteShipLockService] })`.

`waste-exchange.module.spec.ts` (M1.7, `useMocker` compile test) is never edited by an M2 task; tasks that add providers keep it in their Gate and it must pass unchanged.

Commits: the harness adds the `Co-Authored-By` trailer. Commits stay local; never push.

import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { Logger } from '@nestjs/common';
import { WasteConfigService } from './waste-config.service';
import { WASTE_CONFIG_KEYS } from '../waste-config.keys';
import type { SystemConfigService } from '../../system-config/system-config.service';

function makeConfig(values: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
  const get = jest.fn(async (key: string, fallback?: unknown) => (key in values ? values[key] : fallback));
  return { svc: new WasteConfigService({ get } as unknown as SystemConfigService), get };
}
const FEE = { zones: [], defaultZone: { name: 'Toàn quốc', low: 30000, high: 85000 } };
const THRESHOLDS = {
  quotaPacePct: 60, quotaPaceDays: 10, fullMatchPct: 95, fullMatchMinSample: 10, repeatCustomerCount: 3, repeatCustomerDays: 7,
  avgUnitsFactor: 2, lookupMissCount: 10, lookupMissDays: 1, staffDirectCount: 5, staffDirectDays: 7,
};
const DEFAULTS = {
  enabled: false, laneDropoff: true, laneMailin: true, lanePickup: false, mailinMinPoints: 15, pickupMinPoints: 10,
  customerMonthlyCapPoints: 60, maxOpenRequests: 3, requestHoldDays: 7, pickupWaitDays: 14, inTransitReviewDays: 30,
  mailinMaxWeightKg: 20, mailinMaxEdgeCm: 50, shortfallLockPct: 50, shortfallLockStreak: 3, staffDailyCapPoints: 1000,
  giftClaimDays: 60, pointInactiveDays: 60, mailinFeeTable: FEE, alertThresholds: THRESHOLDS, rulesText: '',
};

describe('WasteConfigService.snapshot', () => {
  afterEach(() => jest.restoreAllMocks());

  it('no stored rows -> the 21 defaults of spec 11.5', async () => {
    expect(await makeConfig().svc.snapshot()).toEqual(DEFAULTS);
  });

  it('valid stored values pass through', async () => {
    const fee = { zones: [{ name: 'Miền Bắc', provinceCodes: ['01'], low: 35000, high: 90000 }], defaultZone: FEE.defaultZone };
    const { svc } = makeConfig({
      'waste.enabled': true, 'waste.lane_pickup_enabled': true, 'waste.mailin_min_points': 20,
      'waste.customer_monthly_cap_points': 80, 'waste.mailin_fee_table': fee, 'waste.rules_text': 'Thể lệ',
    });
    expect(await svc.snapshot()).toEqual({ ...DEFAULTS, enabled: true, lanePickup: true, mailinMinPoints: 20, customerMonthlyCapPoints: 80, mailinFeeTable: fee, rulesText: 'Thể lệ' });
  });

  it.each([
    ['waste.enabled', 'true', 'enabled', false],
    ['waste.mailin_min_points', 0, 'mailinMinPoints', 15],
    ['waste.mailin_min_points', 1001, 'mailinMinPoints', 15],
    ['waste.max_open_requests', 2.5, 'maxOpenRequests', 3],
    ['waste.in_transit_review_days', 6, 'inTransitReviewDays', 30],

    ['waste.alert_thresholds', { quotaPacePct: 60 }, 'alertThresholds', THRESHOLDS],
    ['waste.rules_text', 42, 'rulesText', ''],
  ])('invalid %s = %p -> default and exactly one warning naming the key', async (key, raw, field, expected) => {
    const warn = jest.spyOn(Logger.prototype, 'warn').mockImplementation(() => undefined);

    const { svc, get } = makeConfig();
    await svc.snapshot();
    expect(get.mock.calls.map((c) => c[0]).sort()).toEqual(Object.keys(WASTE_CONFIG_KEYS).sort());
    for (const [key, fb] of get.mock.calls) expect(fb).toEqual(WASTE_CONFIG_KEYS[key as keyof typeof WASTE_CONFIG_KEYS].default);
    const src = readFileSync(join(__dirname, 'waste-config.service.ts'), 'utf-8');
    const literals = [...src.matchAll(/\.get<unknown>\(\s*'(waste\.[a-z_]+)'/g)].map((m) => m[1]);
    expect([...new Set(literals)].sort()).toEqual(Object.keys(WASTE_CONFIG_KEYS).sort());

});

describe('WasteConfigService.isEnabled', () => {
  it.each([[true, true], [false, false], ['true', false], [1, false], [undefined, false]])('waste.enabled = %p -> %p', async (raw, expected) => {
    const { svc, get } = makeConfig(raw === undefined ? {} : { 'waste.enabled': raw });
    await expect(svc.isEnabled()).resolves.toBe(expected);
    expect(get).toHaveBeenCalledWith('waste.enabled', false);
  });
});
```

Append to `apps/api/src/modules/waste-exchange/waste-config.keys.spec.ts` (add `readdirSync`, `statSync` to its `node:fs` import):

```ts
describe('every waste key has a runtime reader (registry 8.5)', () => {
  it('each key of WASTE_CONFIG_KEYS occurs as a quoted literal in a non-spec source file other than the key descriptor files', () => {
    const SRC = join(__dirname, '../..');
    const walk = (dir: string, out: string[] = []): string[] => {
      for (const name of readdirSync(dir)) {
        const p = join(dir, name);
        if (statSync(p).isDirectory()) walk(p, out);
        else if (name.endsWith('.ts') && !name.endsWith('.spec.ts') && !name.endsWith('.d.ts')) out.push(p);
      }
      return out;
    };
    const text = walk(SRC)
      .filter((f) => !['waste-exchange/waste-config.keys.ts', 'system-config/dead-config-keys.ts', 'system-config/system-config.service.ts', 'admin/admin-config-rules.ts', 'admin/config-redaction.ts'].some((d) => f.endsWith(`/modules/${d}`)))
      .map((f) => readFileSync(f, 'utf-8').replace(/\/\*[\s\S]*?\*\//g, '').replace(/^\s*\/\/.*$/gm, ''))
      .join('\n');
    const missing = Object.keys(WASTE_CONFIG_KEYS).filter((k) => !["'", '"', '`'].some((q) => text.includes(`${q}${k}${q}`)));
    expect(missing).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { computeWastePublicFlags } from './waste-public-config';

const cfg = (values: Record<string, unknown> = {}) => ({
  get: jest.fn(async (key: string, fallback?: unknown) => (key in values ? values[key] : fallback)),
});

describe('computeWastePublicFlags (spec 11.5, 12.4)', () => {
  it('defaults: programme off -> every flag false, Gomdon never asked', async () => {
    const gomdon = jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(true);
    await expect(computeWastePublicFlags(cfg(), gomdon)).resolves.toEqual({ enabled: false, dropoff: false, mailin: false, pickup: false });
    expect(gomdon).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
  it('programme on with default lanes -> dropoff and mailin on, pickup off (lane default false), Gomdon not asked', async () => {
    const gomdon = jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(true);
    await expect(computeWastePublicFlags(cfg({ 'waste.enabled': true }), gomdon)).resolves.toEqual({ enabled: true, dropoff: true, mailin: true, pickup: false });
    expect(gomdon).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
  it('pickup needs the programme, its lane flag and Gomdon', async () => {
    const on = { 'waste.enabled': true, 'waste.lane_pickup_enabled': true };
    await expect(computeWastePublicFlags(cfg(on), async () => true)).resolves.toMatchObject({ pickup: true });
    await expect(computeWastePublicFlags(cfg(on), async () => false)).resolves.toMatchObject({ pickup: false });
    await expect(computeWastePublicFlags(cfg(on), async () => { throw new Error('gomdon'); })).resolves.toMatchObject({ pickup: false });
  });
  it('programme off hides every lane even when the lane flags and Gomdon are on', async () => {
    const all = { 'waste.enabled': false, 'waste.lane_dropoff_enabled': true, 'waste.lane_mailin_enabled': true, 'waste.lane_pickup_enabled': true };
    await expect(computeWastePublicFlags(cfg(all), async () => true)).resolves.toEqual({ enabled: false, dropoff: false, mailin: false, pickup: false });
  });
  it('a lane flag off turns only that lane off', async () => {
    await expect(computeWastePublicFlags(cfg({ 'waste.enabled': true, 'waste.lane_mailin_enabled': false }), async () => true)).resolves.toEqual({ enabled: true, dropoff: true, mailin: false, pickup: false });
  });
  it('only a JSON true counts (the string "true" is off)', async () => {
    await expect(computeWastePublicFlags(cfg({ 'waste.enabled': 'true' }), async () => true)).resolves.toMatchObject({ enabled: false });
  });
  it('reads the four keys as inline literals (registry 8.5)', () => {
    const src = readFileSync(join(__dirname, 'waste-public-config.ts'), 'utf-8');
    for (const k of ['waste.enabled', 'waste.lane_dropoff_enabled', 'waste.lane_mailin_enabled', 'waste.lane_pickup_enabled']) expect(src).toContain(`'${k}'`);
  });
});

import { readdirSync, readFileSync, statSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join, relative } from 'node:path';
import type { Prisma } from '@prisma/client';
import type { PrismaService } from '../../../prisma/prisma.service';
import { WasteEventsService, deviceMetaOf, truncateIp, writeWasteEvent } from './waste-events';

const fakeDb = () => {
  const create = jest.fn().mockResolvedValue({ id: 'ev-1' });
  return { db: { wasteEvent: { create } } as unknown as Prisma.TransactionClient, create };
};

describe('writeWasteEvent', () => {
  it('writes one row, absent ids become null, returns the id', async () => {
    const { db, create } = fakeDb();
    await expect(writeWasteEvent(db, { kind: 'REQUEST_CREATED', actorUserId: 'u1', actorRole: 'CUSTOMER', requestId: 'r1', meta: { lines: [{ itemId: 'i1', declaredQty: 3 }] } })).resolves.toEqual({ id: 'ev-1' });
    expect(create).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
      data: { kind: 'REQUEST_CREATED', actorUserId: 'u1', actorRole: 'CUSTOMER', requestId: 'r1', pointId: null, giftRedemptionId: null, meta: { lines: [{ itemId: 'i1', declaredQty: 3 }] } },
      select: { id: true },
    });
  });
  it('leaves meta out when absent', async () => {
    const { db, create } = fakeDb();
    await writeWasteEvent(db, { kind: 'SHIP_LOCKED', actorUserId: null, actorRole: 'SYSTEM' });
    expect(create.mock.calls[0]![0].data).toEqual({ kind: 'SHIP_LOCKED', actorUserId: null, actorRole: 'SYSTEM', requestId: null, pointId: null, giftRedemptionId: null });
  });
  it('rejects a kind outside WASTE_EVENT_KINDS and writes nothing', async () => {
    const { db, create } = fakeDb();
    await expect(writeWasteEvent(db, { kind: 'NOPE' as never, actorUserId: null, actorRole: 'SYSTEM' })).rejects.toThrow('Unknown waste event kind: NOPE');
    expect(create).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});

describe('truncateIp (spec 5.5: IPv4 drops the last octet, IPv6 keeps 48 bits)', () => {
  it.each([
    ['203.0.113.42', '203.0.113.0'],
    ['::ffff:203.0.113.42', '203.0.113.0'],
    ['2001:0db8:85a3:0000:0000:8a2e:0370:7334', '2001:db8:85a3::'],
    ['2001:db8::1', '2001:db8:0::'],
    ['garbage', null],
    ['', null],
    [null, null],
    [undefined, null],
  ])('%p -> %p', (ip, expected) => expect(truncateIp(ip)).toBe(expected));
});

describe('deviceMetaOf', () => {
  it('truncates the IP and keeps the first 200 characters of the user agent', () => {
    expect(deviceMetaOf({ ip: '203.0.113.42', headers: { 'user-agent': 'x'.repeat(250) } })).toEqual({ ip: '203.0.113.0', userAgent: 'x'.repeat(200) });
  });
  it('takes the first user-agent value of a repeated header; nulls when absent', () => {
    expect(deviceMetaOf({ ip: undefined, headers: { 'user-agent': ['A', 'B'] } })).toEqual({ ip: null, userAgent: 'A' });
    expect(deviceMetaOf({ headers: {} })).toEqual({ ip: null, userAgent: null });
  });
});

describe('WasteEventsService.record', () => {
  it('writes through the passed tx, else through PrismaService', async () => {
    const own = fakeDb();
    const tx = fakeDb();
    const svc = new WasteEventsService(own.db as unknown as PrismaService);
    await svc.record({ kind: 'LOOKUP_MISS', actorUserId: 's1', actorRole: 'POINT_STAFF', pointId: 'p1' });
    await svc.record({ kind: 'LOOKUP_MISS', actorUserId: 's1', actorRole: 'POINT_STAFF', pointId: 'p1' }, tx.db);
    expect(own.create).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(tx.create).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });
});

describe('waste_events is append-only (spec 11.1)', () => {
  const SRC = join(__dirname, '../../..');
  const PRISMA_WRITE = /\.wasteEvent\.(update|updateMany|upsert|delete|deleteMany)\(/;
  const SQL_WRITE = /(UPDATE|DELETE\s+FROM|TRUNCATE(\s+TABLE)?)\s+"?waste_events"?\b/i;
  const walk = (dir: string, out: string[] = []): string[] => {
    for (const name of readdirSync(dir)) {
      const p = join(dir, name);
      if (statSync(p).isDirectory()) walk(p, out);
      else if (name.endsWith('.ts') && !name.endsWith('.spec.ts')) out.push(p);
    }
    return out;
  };
  it('the SQL pattern catches update, delete and truncate but not insert', () => {
    for (const s of ['DELETE FROM waste_events', 'UPDATE "waste_events" SET', 'TRUNCATE waste_events']) expect(s).toMatch(SQL_WRITE);
    expect('INSERT INTO "waste_events"').not.toMatch(SQL_WRITE);
  });
  it('no application file under apps/api/src updates or deletes a waste_events row', () => {
    const offenders = walk(SRC).filter((f) => {
      const s = readFileSync(f, 'utf-8');
      return PRISMA_WRITE.test(s) || SQL_WRITE.test(s);
    });
    expect(offenders.map((f) => relative(SRC, f))).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

import * as F from './waste-queue-filters';

describe('waste queue filters (single source for M3.8, M4.3, M4.6, M4.8)', () => {
  const now = new Date('2026-10-17T03:00:00.000Z');
  it('static filters', () => {
    expect(F.WASTE_TO_COUNT_WHERE).toEqual({ method: { in: ['MAIL_IN', 'PICKUP'] }, status: 'RECEIVED' });
    expect(F.WASTE_EXPIRED_WHERE).toEqual({ status: 'EXPIRED' });
    expect(F.WASTE_UNMATCHED_OPEN_WHERE).toEqual({ status: 'OPEN' });
    expect(F.WASTE_POINTS_PENDING_WHERE).toEqual({ status: 'PENDING' });
    expect(F.WASTE_PERKS_PENDING_WHERE).toEqual({ AND: [{ perkPendingText: { not: null } }, { perkPendingText: { not: '' } }] });
  });
  it('customer blocked: open statuses and users.isBlocked or waste blockedAt', () => {
    expect(F.wasteCustomerBlockedWhere()).toEqual({
      status: { in: ['OPEN', 'IN_TRANSIT', 'RECEIVED'] },
      user: { OR: [{ isBlocked: true }, { wasteFlags: { is: { blockedAt: { not: null } } } }] },
    });
  });
  it('order not delivered: attached OPEN PICKUP whose order is not DELIVERED, RETURNED or CANCELLED', () => {
    expect(F.wasteOrderNotDeliveredWhere()).toEqual({
      method: 'PICKUP', orderId: { not: null }, status: 'OPEN',
      order: { is: { status: { notIn: ['DELIVERED', 'RETURNED', 'CANCELLED'] } } },
    });
  });
  it('transit overdue: MAIL_IN IN_TRANSIT older than reviewDays', () => {
    expect(F.wasteTransitOverdueWhere(now, 30)).toEqual({ method: 'MAIL_IN', status: 'IN_TRANSIT', inTransitAt: { lt: new Date('2026-09-17T03:00:00.000Z') } });
  });
  it('order mismatch: cancelled order with live request, or delivered/returned order with cancelled request', () => {
    expect(F.wasteOrderMismatchWhere()).toEqual({
      OR: [
        { status: { not: 'CANCELLED' }, order: { is: { status: 'CANCELLED' } } },
        { status: 'CANCELLED', order: { is: { status: { in: ['DELIVERED', 'RETURNED'] } } } },
      ],
    });
  });
  it('gift overdue: READY past claimExpiresAt', () => {
    expect(F.wasteGiftOverdueWhere(now)).toEqual({ status: 'READY', claimExpiresAt: { lt: now } });
  });
});

  - `writeWasteEvent(db, input)`: if `!WASTE_EVENT_KINDS.includes(input.kind)` throw `new Error(\`Unknown waste event kind: ${input.kind}\`)`; else `db.wasteEvent.create({ data: { kind, actorUserId, actorRole, requestId: input.requestId ?? null, pointId: input.pointId ?? null, giftRedemptionId: input.giftRedemptionId ?? null, ...(input.meta === undefined ? {} : { meta: input.meta }) }, select: { id: true } })`.
  - `truncateIp`: empty or nullish returns null; strip a leading `::ffff:` when the rest is IPv4; use `isIPv4`/`isIPv6` from `node:net`; IPv4 returns the first three octets plus `.0`; IPv6: split on `::`, pad the middle with `'0'` groups to 8 hextets, take the first 3, strip leading zeros of each (`'0000'` becomes `'0'`), lower-case, join with `:` and append `::`; anything else returns null.
  - `deviceMetaOf`: `ip: truncateIp(req.ip)`; `userAgent`: the header value (first element if an array) sliced to 200 characters, else null.
  - `WasteEventsService`: constructor `(private readonly prisma: PrismaService)`; `record(input, db?)` returns `writeWasteEvent(db ?? this.prisma, input)`.

    ['perkReviewed rejected', (s) => s.perkReviewed('o1', { id: 'p1', name: 'Tạp hoá Cô Ba' }, false, 'ev6'), 'o1', 'WASTE_PERK_REVIEWED', { point: 'Tạp hoá Cô Ba', result: 'chưa được duyệt', point_id: 'p1' }, 'waste-perk:p1:ev6'],
  ])('%s', async (_n, call, user, code, data, dedupeKey) => {
    const { svc, notify } = make();
    await expect(call(svc)).resolves.toBeUndefined();
    expect(notify).toHaveBeenCalledWith(user, code, data, { dedupeKey });
  });

  it('a failing notify is swallowed with one warning', async () => {
    const warn = jest.spyOn(Logger.prototype, 'warn').mockImplementation(() => undefined);
    const { svc } = make(true);
    await expect(svc.pickupCancelled('u1', req)).resolves.toBeUndefined();
    await Promise.resolve();
    expect(warn).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('every rejection and close reason has a text: no full stop at the end, no apostrophe, no voucher, no standalone xu', () => {
    expect(Object.keys(WASTE_REJECTION_REASON_TEXT).sort()).toEqual([...WASTE_REJECTION_REASON_VALUES].sort());
    expect(Object.keys(WASTE_EXPIRED_DETAIL_TEXT).sort()).toEqual([...WASTE_CLOSE_REASON_VALUES].sort());
    for (const t of [...Object.values(WASTE_REJECTION_REASON_TEXT), ...Object.values(WASTE_EXPIRED_DETAIL_TEXT)]) {
      expect(t.endsWith('.')).toBe(false);
      expect(t).not.toContain("'");
      expect(t).not.toMatch(/voucher/i);
      expect(t).not.toMatch(/(?<![\p{L}_])xu(?![\p{L}_])/iu);
    }
  });

  it('pins the rejection and expiry sentences (spec 4.6, 14.1)', () => {
    expect(WASTE_REJECTION_REASON_TEXT).toEqual({ WET: 'hàng bị ướt', DIRTY: 'hàng còn bẩn', FOREIGN_OBJECT: 'có lẫn vật lạ', WRONG_TYPE: 'không đúng loại hàng Tubu nhận', DAMAGED: 'hàng bị hỏng', NOT_ACCEPTED: 'có món Tubu không nhận', OVERSIZE: 'kiện vượt giới hạn cân nặng hoặc kích thước', HAZARDOUS: 'có vật không nhận', UNPAID_POSTAGE: 'kiện còn cước phải trả khi nhận', OTHER: 'lý do khác, Tubu đã ghi chú trong yêu cầu' });
    expect(WASTE_EXPIRED_DETAIL_TEXT).toEqual({ HOLD_EXPIRED: 'hết hạn giữ chỗ, phần trần của bạn đã được trả lại', PICKUP_NO_SHOW: 'kho chưa nhận được rác thu kèm đơn', PICKUP_NOT_DISPATCHED: 'Tubu chưa sắp xếp được người đến lấy rác thu kèm đơn, xin lỗi bạn. Bạn có thể gửi về kho Tubu ở lần sau', LOST: 'đã đóng vì kiện thất lạc trên đường gửi', LOST_AT_WAREHOUSE: 'kiện bị thất lạc tại kho Tubu, xin lỗi bạn. Bạn nhắn Tubu Tree để được hỗ trợ', SHIP_LOCKED: 'đường gửi kho và thu kèm đơn đang tạm dừng', ORDER_CANCELLED: 'đơn mua gắn kèm đã bị huỷ' });
  });

  it('stripTrailingStop removes trailing dots and spaces only', () => {
    expect(stripTrailingStop(' Gian lận số lượng... ')).toBe('Gian lận số lượng');
    expect(stripTrailingStop('Đã kiểm 3.5 kg')).toBe('Đã kiểm 3.5 kg');
  });

  it('passes each of the ten codes as a string literal to notify (notification-templates-seed gate)', () => {
    const src = readFileSync(join(__dirname, 'waste-notifier.service.ts'), 'utf-8');
    for (const code of ['WASTE_PARCEL_RECEIVED', 'WASTE_POINTS_CREDITED', 'WASTE_REQUEST_REJECTED', 'WASTE_REQUEST_EXPIRED', 'WASTE_PICKUP_STARTED', 'WASTE_PICKUP_CANCELLED', 'WASTE_POINTS_REVERSED', 'WASTE_SHIP_LOCKED', 'WASTE_GIFT_CANCELLED', 'WASTE_PERK_REVIEWED']) {
      expect(src).toMatch(new RegExp(`notify\\(\\s*\\w+,\\s*'${code}'`));
    }
  });
});
```

`apps/api/src/common/staff-access.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { ForbiddenException } from '@nestjs/common';
import { assertActiveAdmin, assertActiveStaff } from './staff-access';

const STAFF_MSG = 'Chỉ nhân viên hoặc quản trị viên mới làm được thao tác này.';
const db = (...rows: unknown[]) => {
  const findUnique = jest.fn();
  for (const r of rows) findUnique.mockResolvedValueOnce(r);
  return { db: { user: { findUnique } } as never, findUnique };
};
const row = (over: Record<string, unknown> = {}) => ({ id: 's1', role: 'STAFF', isBlocked: false, mergedIntoId: null, phone: '0901234567', ...over });

describe('assertActiveStaff (spec 15.1 b, c; re-read from DB on every call)', () => {
  it.each(['STAFF', 'ADMIN'])('%s active -> { id, role, phone }', async (role) => {
    const { db: d, findUnique } = db(row({ role }));
    await expect(assertActiveStaff(d, 's1')).resolves.toEqual({ id: 's1', role, phone: '0901234567' });
    expect(findUnique).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ where: { id: 's1' }, select: { id: true, role: true, isBlocked: true, mergedIntoId: true, phone: true } });
  });
  it.each([
    ['customer', row({ role: 'CUSTOMER' })],
    ['dealer', row({ role: 'DEALER' })],
    ['blocked', row({ isBlocked: true })],
    ['merged', row({ mergedIntoId: 'u9' })],
    ['missing', null],
  ])('%s -> 403 with the default sentence', async (_n, r) => {
    const { db: d } = db(r);
    await expect(assertActiveStaff(d, 's1')).rejects.toThrow(new ForbiddenException(STAFF_MSG));
  });
  it('a caller message replaces the default (LoyaltyService keeps its sentence, registry 8.6)', async () => {
    const { db: d } = db(row({ role: 'CUSTOMER' }));
    await expect(assertActiveStaff(d, 's1', 'Chỉ nhân viên hoặc quản trị viên mới có quyền quét thẻ thành viên.')).rejects.toThrow('Chỉ nhân viên hoặc quản trị viên mới có quyền quét thẻ thành viên.');
  });
  it('a staff member demoted between two calls loses access on the second call', async () => {
    const { db: d } = db(row(), row({ role: 'CUSTOMER' }));
    await expect(assertActiveStaff(d, 's1')).resolves.toMatchObject({ role: 'STAFF' });
    await expect(assertActiveStaff(d, 's1')).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ForbiddenException);
  });
});

describe('assertActiveAdmin', () => {
  it('ADMIN passes, STAFF is refused', async () => {
    await expect(assertActiveAdmin(db(row({ role: 'ADMIN' })).db, 's1')).resolves.toMatchObject({ role: 'ADMIN' });
    await expect(assertActiveAdmin(db(row()).db, 's1')).rejects.toThrow(new ForbiddenException('Chỉ quản trị viên mới làm được thao tác này.'));
  });
});
```

`apps/api/src/common/idempotency-key.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { BadRequestException } from '@nestjs/common';
import { parseTreeBuyKey } from '../modules/game/tree-purchase-key';
import { requireIdempotencyKey } from './idempotency-key';

const invalidMessage = (() => { try { parseTreeBuyKey('bad'); } catch (e) { return (e as Error).message; } return ''; })();

describe('requireIdempotencyKey (spec 15.1)', () => {
  it.each(['abcd1234', 'A-b_c-9x', 'a'.repeat(128)])('accepts %p unchanged', (k) => expect(requireIdempotencyKey(k)).toBe(k));
  it.each(['short', 'a'.repeat(129), 'has space1', ' abcdefgh', 'abcdefg!', '', ['abcdefgh', 'x'], 12345678])('rejects %p with the parseTreeBuyKey message', (k) => {
    expect(() => requireIdempotencyKey(k)).toThrow(new BadRequestException(invalidMessage));
  });
  it('a missing header is a 400 too', () => {
    expect(() => requireIdempotencyKey(undefined)).toThrow(new BadRequestException('Thiếu Idempotency-Key: cần 8 đến 128 ký tự gồm chữ cái, chữ số, dấu gạch dưới (_) hoặc gạch nối (-).'));
  });
});
```

`apps/api/src/modules/waste-exchange/core/waste-mask.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { maskPhoneOrNull } from '../../../common/mask-pii';
import { customerView } from './waste-mask';

const u = { fullName: 'Nguyễn Văn An', phone: '0901234567', id: 'u1' };
describe('customerView (spec 15.1: masked unless the re-read role is ADMIN; userId only for ADMIN and when needed)', () => {
  it('non-admin: masked name and phone, never userId', () => {
    expect(customerView(u, false)).toEqual({ name: 'An***', phone: maskPhoneOrNull('0901234567') });
    expect(customerView(u, false, true)).toEqual({ name: 'An***', phone: maskPhoneOrNull('0901234567') });
  });
  it('admin: full values; userId only when asked', () => {
    expect(customerView(u, true)).toEqual({ name: 'Nguyễn Văn An', phone: '0901234567' });
    expect(customerView(u, true, true)).toEqual({ name: 'Nguyễn Văn An', phone: '0901234567', userId: 'u1' });
  });
  it('missing name -> Khách Tubu for both', () => {
    expect(customerView({ ...u, fullName: null }, true).name).toBe('Khách Tubu');
    expect(customerView({ ...u, fullName: null, phone: null }, false)).toEqual({ name: 'Khách Tubu', phone: null });
  });
});
```

import { WASTE_MESSAGES as M } from './waste-messages';

describe('WASTE_MESSAGES (registry 4.13 pinned sentences)', () => {

    expect(M.NEED_PHONE).toBe('Bạn cần chia sẻ số điện thoại để dùng Đổi rác lấy quà.');
    expect(M.BLOCKED).toBe('Tài khoản của bạn tạm không tham gia Đổi rác, liên hệ Tubu Tree.');
    expect(M.SHIP_LOCKED).toBe('Đường gửi kho và thu kèm đơn đang tạm dừng với tài khoản của bạn, liên hệ CSKH.');
    expect(M.POINT_UNAVAILABLE).toBe('Điểm nhận đang tạm ngừng, hãy chọn điểm khác');
    expect(M.ITEM_NOT_ACTIVE).toBe('Món này hiện chưa nhận, hãy bỏ khỏi danh sách');
    expect(M.NOT_MAIL_IN).toBe('Yêu cầu này không dùng mã vận đơn');
    expect(M.RETRY).toBe('Vui lòng thử lại');
  });
  it('builders', () => {
    expect(M.BELOW_MIN('MAIL_IN', 15, 5)).toBe('Gói gửi về kho cần tối thiểu 15 điểm, bạn cần thêm 5 điểm nữa');
    expect(M.BELOW_MIN('PICKUP', 10, 1)).toBe('Thu kèm đơn cần tối thiểu 10 điểm, bạn cần thêm 1 điểm nữa');
    expect(M.MONTH_CAP_FOR_MIN('MAIL_IN')).toBe('Tháng này bạn không đủ trần tháng cho gói gửi kho');
    expect(M.MONTH_CAP_FOR_MIN('PICKUP')).toBe('Tháng này bạn không đủ trần tháng cho gói thu kèm đơn');
    expect(M.ITEM_MONTH_CAP('Thùng carton', 10, 'thùng')).toBe('Thùng carton: tháng này bạn chỉ còn gửi được 10 thùng');
    expect(M.ITEM_DAY_CAP('Thùng carton', 4, 'thùng')).toBe('Thùng carton: hôm nay bạn chỉ còn mang tới điểm nhận được 4 thùng');
    expect(M.POINTS_MONTH_CAP(10)).toBe('Tháng này bạn chỉ còn 10 Điểm Xanh trong trần đổi rác');
    expect(M.MAX_OPEN(3)).toBe('Bạn đang có 3 yêu cầu chưa xong, hãy hoàn tất hoặc huỷ bớt trước khi tạo thêm');
    expect(M.PICKUP_PENDING('TR-AAAAA')).toBe('Bạn đã có yêu cầu thu kèm TR-AAAAA, hãy gắn vào đơn kế tiếp');
    expect(M.POINT_MISSING_ITEMS(['Màng xốp', 'Vỏ hộp sữa'])).toBe('Điểm nhận này không nhận: Màng xốp, Vỏ hộp sữa');
  });
  it('the 19 fixed sentences used by later M2 tasks', () => {
    expect(M).toMatchObject({
      REQUEST_NOT_FOUND: 'Không tìm thấy yêu cầu',
      STAFF_ONLY: 'Chỉ nhân viên hoặc quản trị viên mới làm được thao tác này.',
      ADMIN_ONLY: 'Chỉ quản trị viên mới làm được thao tác này.',
      POINT_NOT_FOUND: 'Không tìm thấy điểm nhận',
      POINT_INACTIVE: 'Điểm nhận này đang không hoạt động',
      ACCOUNT_LOCKED: 'Tài khoản của bạn không thao tác được tại điểm nhận',
      NOT_POINT_STAFF: 'Bạn không phải người ở điểm nhận này',

      LANE_DISABLED: 'Cách giao này đang tạm tắt, hãy chọn cách khác',
      POINT_QUOTA_FULL: 'Điểm nhận đã hết hạn mức tháng này, hãy chọn điểm khác',

      MAILIN_CONSENT: 'Bạn cần đồng ý quy tắc đóng gói và cam kết đã trả đủ cước để gửi về kho',
      IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT: 'Mã chống gửi lặp này đã dùng cho một yêu cầu khác',
      NOT_EDITABLE: 'Yêu cầu này không còn sửa được',
      NOT_CANCELLABLE: 'Yêu cầu này không còn huỷ được',
      TRACKING_CLOSED: 'Yêu cầu này không còn nhập mã vận đơn được',

    });
  });
  it('no apostrophe, no voucher, no standalone xu in any sentence', () => {

      M.BELOW_MIN('MAIL_IN', 15, 5), M.BELOW_MIN('PICKUP', 10, 1), M.MONTH_CAP_FOR_MIN('MAIL_IN'), M.MONTH_CAP_FOR_MIN('PICKUP'),
      M.ITEM_MONTH_CAP('Thùng carton', 10, 'thùng'), M.ITEM_DAY_CAP('Thùng carton', 4, 'thùng'), M.POINTS_MONTH_CAP(10), M.MAX_OPEN(3),
      M.PICKUP_PENDING('TR-AAAAA'), M.POINT_MISSING_ITEMS(['Màng xốp', 'Vỏ hộp sữa']),
    ];
    for (const t of texts) {
      expect(t).not.toContain("'");
      expect(t).not.toMatch(/voucher/i);
      expect(t).not.toMatch(/(?<![\p{L}_])xu(?![\p{L}_])/iu);
    }
  });
});

import { ForbiddenException } from '@nestjs/common';
import { REAL_USER_REQUIRED_MSG } from '../../../common/real-user';
import { assertActiveStaff } from '../../../common/staff-access';
import type { PrismaService } from '../../../prisma/prisma.service';
import { WasteAccessService, eligibilityOf } from './waste-access.service';

jest.mock('../../../common/staff-access', () => {
  const actual = jest.requireActual<typeof import('../../../common/staff-access')>('../../../common/staff-access');
  return { ...actual, assertActiveStaff: jest.fn(actual.assertActiveStaff) };
});

const OFF = 'Tính năng Đổi rác lấy quà đang tắt. Liên hệ quản trị viên.';
const PHONE = 'Bạn cần chia sẻ số điện thoại để dùng Đổi rác lấy quà.';
const BLOCKED = 'Tài khoản của bạn tạm không tham gia Đổi rác, liên hệ Tubu Tree.';
const real = { id: 'u1', phone: '0901234567', zaloId: 'z1', isBlocked: false, mergedIntoId: null, wasteFlags: null };
const guest = { ...real, phone: null, zaloId: 'guest_dev1' };
const noPhone = { ...real, phone: null };
const blocked = { ...real, wasteFlags: { blockedAt: new Date('2026-10-01T00:00:00Z'), shipLockedAt: null } };
const shipLocked = { ...real, wasteFlags: { blockedAt: null, shipLockedAt: new Date('2026-10-02T00:00:00Z') } };
const svcFor = (row: unknown) => {
  const findUnique = jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(row);
  return { svc: new WasteAccessService({ user: { findUnique } } as unknown as PrismaService), findUnique };
};

describe('eligibilityOf', () => {
  it('GUEST before NEED_PHONE; a phone makes a guest_ zaloId real (isRealUser)', () => {
    expect(eligibilityOf({ phone: null, zaloId: 'guest_x' })).toBe('GUEST');
    expect(eligibilityOf({ phone: null, zaloId: null })).toBe('GUEST');
    expect(eligibilityOf({ phone: null, zaloId: 'z1' })).toBe('NEED_PHONE');
    expect(eligibilityOf({ phone: '0901234567', zaloId: 'guest_x' })).toBe('OK');
  });
});

describe('customerGate (spec 15.1; registry 4.13 messages)', () => {
  it.each<[string, unknown, boolean, string]>([
    ['FULL off beats guest', guest, false, OFF],
    ['FULL guest', guest, true, REAL_USER_REQUIRED_MSG],
    ['FULL no phone', noPhone, true, PHONE],
    ['FULL blocked', blocked, true, BLOCKED],
    ['FULL off for an eligible customer', real, false, OFF],
    ['FULL missing user', null, true, REAL_USER_REQUIRED_MSG],
  ])('%s -> 403', async (_n, row, enabled, msg) => {
    await expect(svcFor(row).svc.customerGate('u1', 'FULL', enabled)).rejects.toThrow(new ForbiddenException(msg));
  });
  it('FULL ok returns the context, shipLockedAt included', async () => {
    await expect(svcFor(shipLocked).svc.customerGate('u1', 'FULL', true)).resolves.toEqual({ userId: 'u1', phone: '0901234567', blocked: false, shipLockedAt: new Date('2026-10-02T00:00:00Z'), programmeEnabled: true });
  });
  it('OWN ignores the programme flag and blockedAt (tracking, cancel, own reads)', async () => {
    await expect(svcFor(blocked).svc.customerGate('u1', 'OWN', false)).resolves.toMatchObject({ blocked: true, programmeEnabled: false });
  });
  it.each<[string, unknown, string]>([['guest', guest, REAL_USER_REQUIRED_MSG], ['no phone', noPhone, PHONE]])('OWN %s -> 403', async (_n, row, msg) => {
    await expect(svcFor(row).svc.customerGate('u1', 'OWN', true)).rejects.toThrow(new ForbiddenException(msg));
  });
  it('re-reads the user on every call with the flag columns', async () => {
    const { svc, findUnique } = svcFor(real);
    await svc.customerGate('u1', 'OWN', true);
    await svc.customerGate('u1', 'OWN', true);
    expect(findUnique).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
    expect(findUnique).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ where: { id: 'u1' }, select: { id: true, phone: true, zaloId: true, isBlocked: true, mergedIntoId: true, wasteFlags: { select: { blockedAt: true, shipLockedAt: true } } } });
  });
});

describe('assertPointAccess at the warehouse uses the shared staff check (spec 15.1 c)', () => {
  const caller = { id: 's1', role: 'STAFF', phone: '0901234567', isBlocked: false, mergedIntoId: null, wasteFlags: null };
  const make = (point: unknown) => {
    const db = { wastePoint: { findUnique: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(point) }, user: { findUnique: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(caller) } };
    return { svc: new WasteAccessService(db as unknown as PrismaService), db };
  };
  beforeEach(() => jest.mocked(assertActiveStaff).mockClear());
  it('WAREHOUSE: assertActiveStaff(db, userId) runs once and gives actorRole', async () => {
    const { svc, db } = make({ id: 'wh', kind: 'WAREHOUSE', name: 'Kho Tubu', status: 'ACTIVE', giftHandoverEnabled: false, monthlyQuotaPoints: null });
    await expect(svc.assertPointAccess('s1', 'wh', { programmeEnabled: false })).resolves.toMatchObject({ kind: 'WAREHOUSE', actorRole: 'STAFF', myRole: 'STAFF' });
    expect(assertActiveStaff).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(assertActiveStaff).toHaveBeenCalledWith(db, 's1');
  });
  it('PARTNER with the programme off: 403 and no staff check', async () => {
    const { svc } = make({ id: 'p1', kind: 'PARTNER', name: 'Tạp hoá Cô Ba', status: 'ACTIVE', giftHandoverEnabled: false, monthlyQuotaPoints: 100 });
    await expect(svc.assertPointAccess('s1', 'p1', { programmeEnabled: false })).rejects.toThrow(new ForbiddenException(OFF));
    expect(assertActiveStaff).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});

The file starts with this header (M2.4 and M2.5 extend it; every created id is removed in `afterAll` with `cleanupWaste` then `deleteItData`; the warehouse is never deleted; the local `createUser` keeps the helper's signature and records each id):

```ts
import { randomInt } from 'node:crypto';
import { Test, type TestingModule } from '@nestjs/testing';
import { PrismaModule } from '../../src/prisma/prisma.module';
import { PrismaService } from '../../src/prisma/prisma.service';
import { WasteAccessService } from '../../src/modules/waste-exchange/core/waste-access.service';
import { createUser as createItUser, deleteItData, warmPool } from './helpers';
import { cleanupWaste, createWarehouse, createWastePoint } from './fixtures/waste-fixtures';

let moduleRef: TestingModule;
let prisma: PrismaService;
let access: WasteAccessService;
const userIds: string[] = [];
const pointIds: string[] = [];
const itemIds: string[] = [];
const storefrontIds: string[] = [];
const phone = () => `09${String(randomInt(0, 100_000_000)).padStart(8, '0')}`;
async function createUser(db: PrismaService, data: Parameters<typeof createItUser>[1] = {}) {
  const u = await createItUser(db, data);
  userIds.push(u.id);
  return u;
}
const reg = (p: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof createWastePoint>>) => {
  pointIds.push(p.id);
  if (p.createdStorefront && p.ownerStorefrontId) storefrontIds.push(p.ownerStorefrontId);
};

beforeAll(async () => {
  moduleRef = await Test.createTestingModule({ imports: [PrismaModule], providers: [WasteAccessService] }).compile();
  prisma = moduleRef.get(PrismaService);
  access = moduleRef.get(WasteAccessService);
  await warmPool(prisma);
});

afterAll(async () => {
  await cleanupWaste(prisma, { pointIds, itemIds, storefrontIds });
  await deleteItData(prisma, { userIds });
  await moduleRef.close();
});
```

```ts
describe('WasteAccessService (real Postgres)', () => {
  it('availablePointIds: warehouse and a PARTNER with one valid OWNER are in; every broken owner condition takes the point out (spec 15.1 d)', async () => {
    const wh = await createWarehouse(prisma);
    const mk = async () => { const o = await createUser(prisma, { phone: phone() }); const p = await createWastePoint(prisma, { kind: 'PARTNER', ownerUserId: o.id }); reg(p); return { o, p }; };
    const ok = await mk();
    const ownerBlocked = await mk(); await prisma.user.update({ where: { id: ownerBlocked.o.id }, data: { isBlocked: true } });
    const ownerMerged = await mk(); const other = await createUser(prisma); await prisma.user.update({ where: { id: ownerMerged.o.id }, data: { mergedIntoId: other.id } });
    const ownerWasteBlocked = await mk(); await prisma.wasteUserFlags.create({ data: { userId: ownerWasteBlocked.o.id, blockedAt: new Date() } });
    const storeLost = await mk(); await prisma.storefront.update({ where: { id: storeLost.p.ownerStorefrontId! }, data: { type: 'CTV', isPartner: false } });
    const ownerInactive = await mk(); await prisma.wastePointStaff.updateMany({ where: { pointId: ownerInactive.p.id }, data: { isActive: false } });
    const paused = await mk(); await prisma.wastePoint.update({ where: { id: paused.p.id }, data: { status: 'PAUSED' } });
    const ids = [wh.id, ok.p.id, ownerBlocked.p.id, ownerMerged.p.id, ownerWasteBlocked.p.id, storeLost.p.id, ownerInactive.p.id, paused.p.id];
    const got = await access.availablePointIds(prisma, ids);
    expect([...got].sort()).toEqual([wh.id, ok.p.id].sort());
  });

  it('assertPointAccess: owner and staff at their PARTNER point; outsider 403; programme off 403 at PARTNER only', async () => {
    const owner = await createUser(prisma, { phone: phone() });
    const staff = await createUser(prisma, { phone: phone() });
    const outsider = await createUser(prisma, { phone: phone() });
    const p = await createWastePoint(prisma, { kind: 'PARTNER', ownerUserId: owner.id }); reg(p);
    await prisma.wastePointStaff.create({ data: { pointId: p.id, userId: staff.id, role: 'STAFF' } });
    await expect(access.assertPointAccess(owner.id, p.id, { programmeEnabled: true })).resolves.toMatchObject({ pointId: p.id, kind: 'PARTNER', actorRole: 'POINT_STAFF', myRole: 'OWNER' });
    await expect(access.assertPointAccess(staff.id, p.id, { programmeEnabled: true })).resolves.toMatchObject({ actorRole: 'POINT_STAFF', myRole: 'STAFF' });
    await expect(access.assertPointAccess(outsider.id, p.id, { programmeEnabled: true })).rejects.toThrow('Bạn không phải người ở điểm nhận này');
    await expect(access.assertPointAccess(owner.id, p.id, { programmeEnabled: false })).rejects.toThrow('Tính năng Đổi rác lấy quà đang tắt. Liên hệ quản trị viên.');
    const wh = await createWarehouse(prisma);
    const whStaff = await createUser(prisma, { role: 'STAFF' });
    await expect(access.assertPointAccess(whStaff.id, wh.id, { programmeEnabled: false })).resolves.toMatchObject({ kind: 'WAREHOUSE', actorRole: 'STAFF', myRole: 'STAFF' });
    await expect(access.assertPointAccess(outsider.id, 'nope', { programmeEnabled: true })).rejects.toThrow('Không tìm thấy điểm nhận');
  });

  it('a PARTNER whose OWNER lost the partner store is 403 for everyone at the point and leaves listOperablePoints (spec 17.3 permission list)', async () => {
    const owner = await createUser(prisma, { phone: phone() });
    const staff = await createUser(prisma, { phone: phone() });
    const p = await createWastePoint(prisma, { kind: 'PARTNER', ownerUserId: owner.id }); reg(p);
    await prisma.wastePointStaff.create({ data: { pointId: p.id, userId: staff.id, role: 'STAFF' } });
    await prisma.storefront.update({ where: { id: p.ownerStorefrontId! }, data: { type: 'CTV', isPartner: false } });
    for (const u of [owner, staff]) {
      await expect(access.assertPointAccess(u.id, p.id, { programmeEnabled: true })).rejects.toThrow('Điểm nhận này đang chờ Tubu kiểm tra chủ điểm, tạm chưa thao tác được');
      expect((await access.listOperablePoints(u.id, true)).map((x) => x.pointId)).not.toContain(p.id);
    }
  });

  it('demoted or blocked warehouse staff loses access on the next call', async () => {
    const wh = await createWarehouse(prisma);
    const s = await createUser(prisma, { role: 'STAFF' });
    await expect(access.assertPointAccess(s.id, wh.id, { programmeEnabled: true })).resolves.toMatchObject({ actorRole: 'STAFF' });
    expect((await access.listOperablePoints(s.id, false)).map((x) => x.pointId)).toEqual([wh.id]);
    await prisma.user.update({ where: { id: s.id }, data: { role: 'CUSTOMER' } });
    await expect(access.assertPointAccess(s.id, wh.id, { programmeEnabled: true })).rejects.toThrow('Chỉ nhân viên hoặc quản trị viên mới làm được thao tác này.');
    expect(await access.listOperablePoints(s.id, true)).toEqual([]);
    const a = await createUser(prisma, { role: 'ADMIN' });
    await prisma.wasteUserFlags.create({ data: { userId: a.id, blockedAt: new Date() } });
    await expect(access.assertWarehouseActor(a.id)).resolves.toMatchObject({ role: 'ADMIN' });
    const b = await createUser(prisma, { role: 'STAFF' });
    await prisma.wasteUserFlags.create({ data: { userId: b.id, blockedAt: new Date() } });
    await expect(access.assertWarehouseActor(b.id)).rejects.toThrow('Tài khoản của bạn tạm không tham gia Đổi rác, liên hệ Tubu Tree.');
  });

  it('assertPointAccess: waste-blocked, blocked or merged caller, paused point; ADMIN exempt from blockedAt (spec 15.1 a, b)', async () => {
    const ok = { programmeEnabled: true };
    const owner = await createUser(prisma, { phone: phone() });
    const staff = await createUser(prisma, { phone: phone() });
    const merged = await createUser(prisma, { phone: phone() });
    const p = await createWastePoint(prisma, { kind: 'PARTNER', ownerUserId: owner.id }); reg(p);
    await prisma.wastePointStaff.createMany({ data: [{ pointId: p.id, userId: staff.id, role: 'STAFF' }, { pointId: p.id, userId: merged.id, role: 'STAFF' }] });
    await prisma.wasteUserFlags.create({ data: { userId: staff.id, blockedAt: new Date() } });
    await expect(access.assertPointAccess(staff.id, p.id, ok)).rejects.toThrow('Tài khoản của bạn tạm không tham gia Đổi rác, liên hệ Tubu Tree.');
    await prisma.user.update({ where: { id: staff.id }, data: { isBlocked: true } });
    await expect(access.assertPointAccess(staff.id, p.id, ok)).rejects.toThrow('Tài khoản của bạn không thao tác được tại điểm nhận');
    await prisma.user.update({ where: { id: merged.id }, data: { mergedIntoId: owner.id } });
    await expect(access.assertPointAccess(merged.id, p.id, ok)).rejects.toThrow('Tài khoản của bạn không thao tác được tại điểm nhận');
    await prisma.wastePoint.update({ where: { id: p.id }, data: { status: 'PAUSED' } });
    await expect(access.assertPointAccess(owner.id, p.id, ok)).rejects.toThrow('Điểm nhận này đang không hoạt động');
    const wh = await createWarehouse(prisma);
    const admin = await createUser(prisma, { role: 'ADMIN' });
    await prisma.wasteUserFlags.create({ data: { userId: admin.id, blockedAt: new Date() } });
    await expect(access.assertPointAccess(admin.id, wh.id, ok)).resolves.toMatchObject({ kind: 'WAREHOUSE', actorRole: 'ADMIN' });
  });
});

```sql
SELECT p.id FROM waste_points p
WHERE p.status::text = 'ACTIVE'
  AND (${ids}::text[] IS NULL OR p.id = ANY(${ids}::text[]))
  AND (p.kind::text = 'WAREHOUSE' OR (
    SELECT count(*) FROM waste_point_staff s
    JOIN users u ON u.id = s."userId"
    LEFT JOIN waste_user_flags f ON f."userId" = u.id
    WHERE s."pointId" = p.id AND s.role::text = 'OWNER' AND s."isActive"
      AND NOT u."isBlocked" AND u."mergedIntoId" IS NULL AND f."blockedAt" IS NULL
      AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM storefronts sf WHERE sf."ownerUserId" = u.id AND (sf.type::text = 'MERCHANT' OR sf."isPartner"))
  ) = 1)
```

    (`ids` = `pointIds ?? null`; the storefront condition is `PARTNER_STORE_WHERE`, `apps/api/src/modules/merchant/partner-store.ts:9`.)

WAREHOUSE: `const staff = await assertActiveStaff(db, userId)` (spec 15.1 (c): the shared helper, never an inline role check; it throws 403 STAFF_ONLY); `actorRole = staff.role`, `myRole = 'STAFF'`. PARTNER: `!programmeEnabled` gives 403 PROGRAMME_OFF; `wastePointStaff.findUnique({ where: { pointId_userId: { pointId, userId } } })` must exist with `isActive`, else 403 NOT_POINT_STAFF; `(await this.availablePointIds(db, [pointId])).has(pointId)` else 403 POINT_OWNER_INVALID; `actorRole = 'POINT_STAFF'`, `myRole = row.role`. `caller = { id, phone, role }`.

```ts
import { readdirSync, readFileSync, statSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { checkCaps, remainingQty } from './waste-caps';

const items = new Map([
  ['carton', { id: 'carton', name: 'Thùng carton', unitLabel: 'thùng', dailyCapQty: 20, monthlyCapQty: 60 }],
  ['film', { id: 'film', name: 'Màng xốp', unitLabel: 'túi', dailyCapQty: null, monthlyCapQty: 10 }],
]);
const usage = (o: Partial<{ day: [string, number][]; month: [string, number][]; points: number }> = {}) => ({
  itemDay: new Map(o.day ?? []), itemMonth: new Map(o.month ?? []), pointsMonth: o.points ?? 0, openCount: 0, pickupPendingCode: null,
});

describe('remainingQty', () => {
  it('null cap = unlimited; never negative', () => {
    expect(remainingQty(null, 99)).toBeNull();
    expect(remainingQty(20, 5)).toBe(15);
    expect(remainingQty(20, 25)).toBe(0);
  });
});

describe('checkCaps (spec 11.2; 5.3)', () => {
  const base = { items, monthlyCapPoints: 60, checkDay: true };
  it('exactly at every cap passes', () => {
    expect(checkCaps({ ...base, lines: [{ itemId: 'carton', declaredQty: 15 }], usage: usage({ day: [['carton', 5]], month: [['carton', 45]], points: 45 }), estimatedPoints: 15 })).toEqual([]);
  });
  it('day cap first, then month cap, then points cap, all reported', () => {
    expect(checkCaps({ ...base, lines: [{ itemId: 'carton', declaredQty: 16 }], usage: usage({ day: [['carton', 5]], month: [['carton', 45]], points: 45 }), estimatedPoints: 16 })).toEqual([
      { code: 'ITEM_DAY_CAP', itemId: 'carton', remaining: 15 },
      { code: 'ITEM_MONTH_CAP', itemId: 'carton', remaining: 15 },
      { code: 'POINTS_MONTH_CAP', remaining: 15 },
    ]);
  });
  it('checkDay false skips the day cap (MAIL_IN, PICKUP, REOPEN)', () => {
    expect(checkCaps({ ...base, checkDay: false, lines: [{ itemId: 'carton', declaredQty: 21 }], usage: usage(), estimatedPoints: 21 })).toEqual([]);
  });
  it('an item without a day cap is limited by month only', () => {
    expect(checkCaps({ ...base, lines: [{ itemId: 'film', declaredQty: 11 }], usage: usage(), estimatedPoints: 0 })).toEqual([{ code: 'ITEM_MONTH_CAP', itemId: 'film', remaining: 10 }]);
  });
});

describe('waste code reads calendar time through common/vn-time.ts (spec 15.1)', () => {
  const walk = (dir: string, out: string[] = []): string[] => {
    for (const name of readdirSync(dir)) {
      const p = join(dir, name);
      if (statSync(p).isDirectory()) walk(p, out);
      else if (name.endsWith('.ts') && !name.endsWith('.spec.ts')) out.push(p);
    }
    return out;
  };
  it('no non-spec file under modules/waste-exchange calls Date setHours or getMonth', () => {
    const root = join(__dirname, '..');
    const offenders = walk(root).filter((f) => /\.(setHours|getMonth)\(/.test(readFileSync(f, 'utf-8')));
    expect(offenders).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { BadRequestException, ConflictException, ForbiddenException } from '@nestjs/common';
import { WasteHoldService, type WasteCapUsage, type WasteHoldInput } from './waste-hold.service';

const NOW = new Date('2026-10-17T03:00:00.000Z');
const CFG = { laneDropoff: true, laneMailin: true, lanePickup: true, mailinMinPoints: 15, pickupMinPoints: 10, customerMonthlyCapPoints: 60, maxOpenRequests: 3, requestHoldDays: 7 };
const carton = { id: 'carton', code: 'CARTON', name: 'Thùng carton', unitLabel: 'thùng', unitsPerPoint: 1, pointsPerGroup: 1, dropsPerUnit: 2, gramsPerUnit: 300, dailyCapQty: 20, monthlyCapQty: 60, status: 'ACTIVE', hazardous: false };
const battery = { ...carton, id: 'pin', code: 'PIN', name: 'Pin cũ', unitLabel: 'viên', status: 'COMING_SOON', hazardous: true };
function setup(o: { items?: unknown[]; storedLines?: unknown[]; flags?: unknown; partner?: unknown; links?: string[]; usageRow?: unknown; usage?: Partial<WasteCapUsage>; available?: boolean } = {}) {
  const tx = {
    wasteItem: { findMany: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(o.items ?? [carton]) },
    wasteRequestLine: { findMany: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(o.storedLines ?? ((o.items ?? [carton]) as (typeof carton)[]).map((i) => ({ itemId: i.id, unitsPerPoint: i.unitsPerPoint, pointsPerGroup: i.pointsPerGroup, dropsPerUnit: i.dropsPerUnit, gramsPerUnit: i.gramsPerUnit }))) },
    wasteUserFlags: { findUnique: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(o.flags ?? null) },
    wastePoint: { findUnique: jest.fn(async ({ where }: { where: { id: string } }) => (where.id === 'wh' ? { id: 'wh', kind: 'WAREHOUSE', monthlyQuotaPoints: null } : (o.partner === undefined ? { id: where.id, kind: 'PARTNER', monthlyQuotaPoints: 100 } : o.partner))) },
    wastePointItem: { findMany: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue((o.links ?? ['carton']).map((itemId) => ({ itemId }))) },
    wastePointUsage: { findUnique: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(o.usageRow ?? null) },
  };
  const access = { availablePointIds: jest.fn(async (_db: unknown, ids: string[]) => new Set(o.available === false ? [] : ids)), warehousePoint: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue({ id: 'wh' }) };
  const svc = new WasteHoldService(access as never);
  jest.spyOn(svc, 'capUsage').mockResolvedValue({ itemDay: new Map(), itemMonth: new Map(), pointsMonth: 0, openCount: 0, pickupPendingCode: null, ...o.usage });
  return { svc, tx: tx as never, access, raw: tx };
}
const input = (o: Partial<WasteHoldInput> = {}): WasteHoldInput => ({ path: 'CREATE', userId: 'u1', method: 'DROP_OFF', lines: [{ itemId: 'carton', declaredQty: 5 }], pointId: 'p1', now: NOW, ...o });
const existing = { id: 'r1', pointId: 'p1', capDayKey: '2026-10-15', capMonthKey: '2026-10' };

describe('WasteHoldService.validateAndHold', () => {
  it('CREATE DROP_OFF: snapshot lines, totals, VN cap keys, expiresAt = now + requestHoldDays', async () => {
    const { svc, tx } = setup();
    await expect(svc.validateAndHold(tx, input(), CFG)).resolves.toEqual({
      pointId: 'p1', capDayKey: '2026-10-17', capMonthKey: '2026-10', expiresAt: new Date('2026-10-24T03:00:00.000Z'),
      lines: [{ itemId: 'carton', itemCode: 'CARTON', itemName: 'Thùng carton', unitLabel: 'thùng', unitsPerPoint: 1, pointsPerGroup: 1, dropsPerUnit: 2, gramsPerUnit: 300, declaredQty: 5, hazardous: false }],
      estimatedPoints: 5, estimatedDrops: 10,
    });
  });
  it('MAIL_IN and PICKUP resolve the warehouse; the body pointId is ignored', async () => {
    const { svc, tx } = setup();
    await expect(svc.validateAndHold(tx, input({ method: 'MAIL_IN', pointId: 'p9', lines: [{ itemId: 'carton', declaredQty: 15 }] }), CFG)).resolves.toMatchObject({ pointId: 'wh' });
  });
  it.each<[string, Parameters<typeof setup>[0], Partial<WasteHoldInput>, unknown]>([
    ['duplicate itemId lines', {}, { lines: [{ itemId: 'carton', declaredQty: 1 }, { itemId: 'carton', declaredQty: 2 }] }, new BadRequestException('Mỗi món chỉ khai một dòng')],
    ['DROP_OFF without point', {}, { pointId: null }, new BadRequestException('Hãy chọn điểm nhận')],
    ['over 2000 points', { items: [{ ...carton, pointsPerGroup: 1000, dailyCapQty: null, monthlyCapQty: null }] }, { lines: [{ itemId: 'carton', declaredQty: 3 }] }, new ConflictException('Một yêu cầu tối đa 2000 điểm, hãy tách thành nhiều yêu cầu')],
    ['blockedAt', { flags: { blockedAt: NOW, shipLockedAt: null } }, {}, new ForbiddenException('Tài khoản của bạn tạm không tham gia Đổi rác, liên hệ Tubu Tree.')],
    ['ship-locked MAIL_IN', { flags: { blockedAt: null, shipLockedAt: NOW } }, { method: 'MAIL_IN', lines: [{ itemId: 'carton', declaredQty: 15 }] }, new ForbiddenException('Đường gửi kho và thu kèm đơn đang tạm dừng với tài khoản của bạn, liên hệ CSKH.')],
    ['lane off', {}, {}, new ConflictException('Cách giao này đang tạm tắt, hãy chọn cách khác')],
    ['max open', { usage: { openCount: 3 } }, {}, new ConflictException('Bạn đang có 3 yêu cầu chưa xong, hãy hoàn tất hoặc huỷ bớt trước khi tạo thêm')],
    ['second unattached PICKUP', { usage: { pickupPendingCode: 'TR-AAAAA' } }, { method: 'PICKUP', lines: [{ itemId: 'carton', declaredQty: 10 }] }, new ConflictException('Bạn đã có yêu cầu thu kèm TR-AAAAA, hãy gắn vào đơn kế tiếp')],
    ['battery line (hazardous, never ACTIVE)', { items: [carton, battery], links: ['carton', 'pin'] }, { lines: [{ itemId: 'carton', declaredQty: 1 }, { itemId: 'pin', declaredQty: 1 }] }, new ConflictException('Món này hiện chưa nhận, hãy bỏ khỏi danh sách')],
    ['unknown item id', { items: [] }, { lines: [{ itemId: 'gone', declaredQty: 1 }] }, new ConflictException('Món này hiện chưa nhận, hãy bỏ khỏi danh sách')],
    ['point unavailable', { available: false }, {}, new ConflictException('Điểm nhận đang tạm ngừng, hãy chọn điểm khác')],
    ['quota full', { usageRow: { pointsUsed: 100 } }, {}, new ConflictException('Điểm nhận đã hết hạn mức tháng này, hãy chọn điểm khác')],
    ['point lacks an item', { links: [] }, {}, new ConflictException('Điểm nhận này không nhận: Thùng carton')],
    ['day cap DROP_OFF', { usage: { itemDay: new Map([['carton', 16]]) } }, {}, new ConflictException('Thùng carton: hôm nay bạn chỉ còn mang tới điểm nhận được 4 thùng')],
    ['month cap', { usage: { itemMonth: new Map([['carton', 58]]) } }, {}, new ConflictException('Thùng carton: tháng này bạn chỉ còn gửi được 2 thùng')],
    ['points cap', { usage: { pointsMonth: 57 } }, {}, new ConflictException('Tháng này bạn chỉ còn 3 Điểm Xanh trong trần đổi rác')],
    ['MAIL_IN below min', {}, { method: 'MAIL_IN', lines: [{ itemId: 'carton', declaredQty: 10 }] }, new ConflictException('Gói gửi về kho cần tối thiểu 15 điểm, bạn cần thêm 5 điểm nữa')],
    ['PICKUP below min', {}, { method: 'PICKUP', lines: [{ itemId: 'carton', declaredQty: 9 }] }, new ConflictException('Thu kèm đơn cần tối thiểu 10 điểm, bạn cần thêm 1 điểm nữa')],
    ['MAIL_IN min does not fit the month cap', { usage: { pointsMonth: 50 } }, { method: 'MAIL_IN', lines: [{ itemId: 'carton', declaredQty: 5 }] }, new ConflictException('Tháng này bạn không đủ trần tháng cho gói gửi kho')],
  ])('CREATE %s', async (name, o, inp, err) => {
    const { svc, tx } = setup(o);
    const cfg = name === 'lane off' ? { ...CFG, laneDropoff: false } : CFG;
    const call = svc.validateAndHold(tx, input(inp), cfg);
    await expect(call).rejects.toThrow(err as Error);
    await expect(call).rejects.toBeInstanceOf((err as Error).constructor);
  });
  it('the quota hint reads the usage row by (pointId, VN month key)', async () => {
    const { svc, tx, raw } = setup({ usageRow: { pointsUsed: 100 } });
    await expect(svc.validateAndHold(tx, input(), CFG)).rejects.toThrow('Điểm nhận đã hết hạn mức tháng này, hãy chọn điểm khác');
    expect(raw.wastePointUsage.findUnique).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ where: { pointId_monthKey: { pointId: 'p1', monthKey: '2026-10' } }, select: { pointsUsed: true } });
  });
  it('day cap is not checked for MAIL_IN (only DROP_OFF)', async () => {
    const { svc, tx } = setup({ usage: { itemDay: new Map([['carton', 20]]) } });
    await expect(svc.validateAndHold(tx, input({ method: 'MAIL_IN', lines: [{ itemId: 'carton', declaredQty: 15 }] }), CFG)).resolves.toMatchObject({ estimatedPoints: 15 });
  });
  it('an unknown point id or a missing warehouse is 409 POINT_UNAVAILABLE, never a 500', async () => {
    const a = setup({ available: false, partner: null });
    const call = a.svc.validateAndHold(a.tx, input({ pointId: 'nope' }), CFG);
    await expect(call).rejects.toThrow(new ConflictException('Điểm nhận đang tạm ngừng, hãy chọn điểm khác'));
    await expect(call).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ConflictException);
    const b = setup({ available: false, partner: null });
    await expect(b.svc.evaluate(b.tx, input({ pointId: 'nope' }), CFG)).resolves.toMatchObject({ blocks: [{ code: 'POINT_UNAVAILABLE' }] });
    const c = setup();
    c.access.warehousePoint.mockResolvedValue(null);
    await expect(c.svc.validateAndHold(c.tx, input({ method: 'MAIL_IN', pointId: null, lines: [{ itemId: 'carton', declaredQty: 15 }] }), CFG)).rejects.toThrow('Điểm nhận đang tạm ngừng, hãy chọn điểm khác');
  });
  it('a PARTNER hidden from the customer lists for inactivity is still accepted on CREATE (spec 5.5: hiding affects only the two customer lists)', async () => {
    const { svc, tx, access } = setup({ partner: { id: 'p1', kind: 'PARTNER', monthlyQuotaPoints: 100, lastConfirmedAt: new Date('2026-07-01T00:00:00Z'), activatedAt: new Date('2026-06-01T00:00:00Z') } });
    await expect(svc.validateAndHold(tx, input(), CFG)).resolves.toMatchObject({ pointId: 'p1' });
    expect(access.availablePointIds).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  describe('EDIT path matrix (spec 11.2; 17.3 permission list)', () => {

      const { svc, tx } = setup({ usage: { openCount: 3 } });

      expect(r).toMatchObject({ pointId: 'wh', capDayKey: '2026-10-15', capMonthKey: '2026-10', expiresAt: null });

      const day = setup({ usage: { itemDay: new Map([['carton', 18]]) } });

      const min = setup();

      const pts = setup({ usage: { pointsMonth: 57 } });

describe('WasteHoldService.evaluate', () => {
  it('collects every block in check order with DTO fields, never throws for caps', async () => {
    const { svc, tx } = setup({ usage: { openCount: 3, pickupPendingCode: 'TR-BBBBB', itemMonth: new Map([['carton', 58]]), pointsMonth: 51 } });
    const { blocks, result } = await svc.evaluate(tx, input({ method: 'PICKUP', pointId: null, lines: [{ itemId: 'carton', declaredQty: 4 }] }), { ...CFG, lanePickup: false });
    expect(blocks).toEqual([
      { code: 'LANE_DISABLED' }, { code: 'MAX_OPEN', openCount: 3 }, { code: 'PICKUP_PENDING', pickupRequestCode: 'TR-BBBBB' },
      { code: 'ITEM_MONTH_CAP', itemId: 'carton' }, { code: 'MONTH_CAP_FOR_MIN', pointsRemaining: 9 },
    ]);
    expect(result).toMatchObject({ pointId: 'wh', estimatedPoints: 4, estimatedDrops: 8 });
  });
  it('DROP_OFF without a point skips the point checks and returns pointId empty', async () => {
    const { svc, tx } = setup();
    await expect(svc.evaluate(tx, input({ pointId: null }), CFG)).resolves.toMatchObject({ blocks: [], result: { pointId: '' } });
  });
  it('BELOW_MIN carries missingPoints; POINTS_MONTH_CAP carries pointsRemaining; ship lock is a block', async () => {
    const { svc, tx } = setup({ flags: { blockedAt: null, shipLockedAt: NOW }, usage: { pointsMonth: 40 } });
    const { blocks } = await svc.evaluate(tx, input({ method: 'MAIL_IN', pointId: null, lines: [{ itemId: 'carton', declaredQty: 12 }] }), CFG);
    expect(blocks).toEqual([{ code: 'SHIP_LOCKED' }, { code: 'BELOW_MIN', missingPoints: 3 }]);
    const p = setup({ usage: { pointsMonth: 58 } });
    expect((await p.svc.evaluate(p.tx, input(), CFG)).blocks).toEqual([{ code: 'POINTS_MONTH_CAP', pointsRemaining: 2 }]);
  });
  it('item and point blocks carry their DTO fields in COLLECT mode', async () => {
    const day = setup({ usage: { itemDay: new Map([['carton', 18]]) } });
    expect((await day.svc.evaluate(day.tx, input(), CFG)).blocks).toEqual([{ code: 'ITEM_DAY_CAP', itemId: 'carton' }]);
    const pin = setup({ items: [carton, battery], links: ['carton', 'pin'] });
    expect((await pin.svc.evaluate(pin.tx, input({ lines: [{ itemId: 'carton', declaredQty: 1 }, { itemId: 'pin', declaredQty: 1 }] }), CFG)).blocks).toEqual([{ code: 'ITEM_NOT_ACTIVE', itemId: 'pin' }]);
    const gone = setup({ available: false });
    expect((await gone.svc.evaluate(gone.tx, input(), CFG)).blocks).toEqual([{ code: 'POINT_UNAVAILABLE' }]);
    const full = setup({ usageRow: { pointsUsed: 100 } });
    expect((await full.svc.evaluate(full.tx, input(), CFG)).blocks).toEqual([{ code: 'POINT_QUOTA_FULL' }]);
    const missing = setup({ links: [] });
    expect((await missing.svc.evaluate(missing.tx, input(), CFG)).blocks).toEqual([{ code: 'POINT_MISSING_ITEMS', itemId: 'carton' }]);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement `WasteHoldService` in `apps/api/src/modules/waste-exchange/core/waste-hold.service.ts`.** Constructor `(private readonly access: WasteAccessService)`. One private `check(tx, input, cfg, mode: 'THROW' | 'COLLECT')` returns `{ result, blocks }` where each block carries its HTTP status and sentence; `validateAndHold` throws the first block (`ForbiddenException` for SHIP_LOCKED, `ConflictException` otherwise) and adds `expiresAt`; `evaluate` maps blocks to `WastePreviewBlockDTO`: `MAX_OPEN` `{ openCount }`, `PICKUP_PENDING` `{ pickupRequestCode }`, `ITEM_NOT_ACTIVE`/`ITEM_DAY_CAP`/`ITEM_MONTH_CAP` `{ itemId }`, `POINT_MISSING_ITEMS` `{ itemId }` of the first missing item, `POINTS_MONTH_CAP` and `MONTH_CAP_FOR_MIN` `{ pointsRemaining: max(0, customerMonthlyCapPoints - pointsMonth) }`, `BELOW_MIN` `{ missingPoints }`, all others `{ code }` only. A line whose item row is missing counts as not ACTIVE and is left out of the totals.
  - Always thrown, both modes, before any block: duplicate `itemId` in `lines` (400 DUPLICATE_LINE); `existing` missing on EDIT/REOPEN (`Error('existing required')`); DROP_OFF with `pointId` null on CREATE/EDIT in THROW mode (400 NEED_POINT); `estimatedPoints > WASTE_REQUEST_POINTS_MAX` (409 REQUEST_POINTS_MAX); flags `blockedAt` on CREATE/EDIT (403 BLOCKED). Flags are read with `tx.wasteUserFlags.findUnique({ where: { userId }, select: { shipLockedAt: true, blockedAt: true } })`; cfg is never re-read (registry 4.6).
  - Keys: CREATE `vnDateKey(now)`/`vnMonthKey(now)`; EDIT/REOPEN the `existing` keys; `capUsage(tx, userId, dayKey, monthKey, existing?.id)`.
  - Point: DROP_OFF uses `input.pointId`; MAIL_IN/PICKUP use `(await this.access.warehousePoint(tx))?.id` (null gives POINT_UNAVAILABLE). Point availability and quota run on CREATE, and on EDIT only when the resolved id differs from `existing.pointId`. Availability is `availablePointIds` only; `isHiddenInactive` (the customer-list rule of spec 5.5) is never applied here. The point row is read with `tx.wastePoint.findUnique({ where: { id: pointId }, select: { id: true, kind: true, monthlyQuotaPoints: true } })` only when the resolved `pointId` is not null; a null warehouse (MAIL_IN/PICKUP) or a missing row (an unknown or empty body id) adds POINT_UNAVAILABLE once, in both modes, and skips blocks 7 and 8 for this call. DROP_OFF with `pointId` null in COLLECT mode still skips every point check (no POINT_UNAVAILABLE).
  - Block order and paths (first wins in THROW): 1 SHIP_LOCKED (CREATE, EDIT; method MAIL_IN/PICKUP; `shipLockedAt` not null); 2 LANE_DISABLED (CREATE); 3 MAX_OPEN (CREATE; `openCount >= maxOpenRequests`); 4 PICKUP_PENDING (CREATE, PICKUP; `pickupPendingCode` not null); 5 ITEM_NOT_ACTIVE (CREATE, EDIT; missing row or `status !== 'ACTIVE'`; DTO `itemId` of the first such line); 6 POINT_UNAVAILABLE; 7 POINT_QUOTA_FULL (PARTNER with `monthlyQuotaPoints` not null and usage row `pointsUsed >= monthlyQuotaPoints`, read with `tx.wastePointUsage.findUnique({ where: { pointId_monthKey: { pointId, monthKey: vnMonthKey(now) } }, select: { pointsUsed: true } })`; spec 11.4: a hint, nothing reserved); 8 POINT_MISSING_ITEMS (CREATE, EDIT; PARTNER only, from `waste_point_items`; the warehouse accepts every ACTIVE item, spec 11.1); 9 to 11 `checkCaps` with `checkDay = method === 'DROP_OFF' && path !== 'REOPEN'` (day, month, points); 12 thresholds (CREATE, EDIT; MAIL_IN uses `mailinMinPoints`, PICKUP `pickupMinPoints`): when `estimatedPoints < min`, MONTH_CAP_FOR_MIN if `customerMonthlyCapPoints - pointsMonth < min`, else BELOW_MIN with `missingPoints = min - estimatedPoints`.
  - Lines snapshot from `tx.wasteItem.findMany({ where: { id: { in: ids } } })` (rate columns of the item row at this moment, spec 5.1); totals with `requestTotals` (M1.7); the result keeps the input line order. On REOPEN the rate columns (unitsPerPoint, pointsPerGroup, dropsPerUnit, gramsPerUnit) come from the stored lines `tx.wasteRequestLine.findMany({ where: { requestId: existing.id }, select: { itemId: true, unitsPerPoint: true, pointsPerGroup: true, dropsPerUnit: true, gramsPerUnit: true } })` keyed by itemId, never from the current item row; the item row still gives name, unit label and per-item caps (spec 5.1: rates are snapshotted only at create and at customer edit; spec 11.2: REOPEN adds back its own stored share). A REOPEN input line whose itemId has no stored line throws `new Error('REOPEN line without stored snapshot')` before any cap check. `validateAndHold`'s signature and `WasteHoldInput` do not change.
- [ ] **Step 5b: Implement `capUsage`** in `apps/api/src/modules/waste-exchange/core/waste-hold.service.ts`.
  - `capUsage(db, userId, capDayKey, capMonthKey, excludeRequestId?)`: two `$queryRaw` statements plus two Prisma reads:

```sql
SELECT l."itemId" AS "itemId",
  COALESCE(SUM(COALESCE(l."receivedQty", l."declaredQty")) FILTER (WHERE r."capDayKey" = ${capDayKey}), 0)::int AS day,
  COALESCE(SUM(COALESCE(l."receivedQty", l."declaredQty")) FILTER (WHERE r."capMonthKey" = ${capMonthKey}), 0)::int AS month
FROM waste_request_lines l JOIN waste_requests r ON r.id = l."requestId"
WHERE r."userId" = ${userId} AND r.status::text IN (${Prisma.join([...WASTE_CAP_STATUSES])})
  AND (r."capDayKey" = ${capDayKey} OR r."capMonthKey" = ${capMonthKey})
  AND (${excludeRequestId ?? null}::text IS NULL OR r.id <> ${excludeRequestId ?? null}::text)
GROUP BY l."itemId";
SELECT COALESCE(SUM(COALESCE(r."pointsAwarded", r."estimatedPoints")), 0)::int AS points
FROM waste_requests r
WHERE r."userId" = ${userId} AND r.status::text IN (${Prisma.join([...WASTE_CAP_STATUSES])}) AND r."capMonthKey" = ${capMonthKey}
  AND (${excludeRequestId ?? null}::text IS NULL OR r.id <> ${excludeRequestId ?? null}::text);
```

    then `openCount = db.wasteRequest.count({ where: { userId, status: { in: [...WASTE_OPEN_STATUSES] } } })` and `pickupPendingCode = (await db.wasteRequest.findFirst({ where: { userId, method: 'PICKUP', status: 'OPEN', orderId: null }, select: { code: true }, orderBy: { createdAt: 'asc' } }))?.code ?? null`.

Items created in a case are pushed to `itemIds`.

```ts
describe('WasteHoldService.capUsage (real Postgres)', () => {
  it('counts COALESCE(received, declared) over OPEN..REVERSED, skips CANCELLED/REJECTED/EXPIRED, excludes the given request; day keys across all methods', async () => {
    const u = await createUser(prisma, { phone: phone() });
    const item = await createWasteItem(prisma, { dailyCapQty: 20, monthlyCapQty: 60 }); itemIds.push(item.id);
    const wh = await createWarehouse(prisma);
    const mk = (method: 'DROP_OFF' | 'MAIL_IN' | 'PICKUP', status: string, declaredQty: number, receivedQty?: number) =>
      createWasteRequest(prisma, { userId: u.id, method, status: status as never, pointId: wh.id, lines: [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty, receivedQty }] });
    const open = await mk('DROP_OFF', 'OPEN', 3);
    await mk('MAIL_IN', 'IN_TRANSIT', 4);
    await mk('MAIL_IN', 'COMPLETED', 9, 5);
    await mk('DROP_OFF', 'REVERSED', 2, 2);
    for (const s of ['CANCELLED', 'REJECTED', 'EXPIRED']) await mk('DROP_OFF', s, 7);
    const today = vnDateKey(); const month = vnMonthKey();
    const all = await hold.capUsage(prisma, u.id, today, month);
    expect(all.itemDay.get(item.id)).toBe(14);
    expect(all.itemMonth.get(item.id)).toBe(14);
    expect(all.openCount).toBe(2);
    const without = await hold.capUsage(prisma, u.id, today, month, open.id);
    expect(without.itemMonth.get(item.id)).toBe(11);
    expect(all.pointsMonth).toBe(18);
    expect(without.pointsMonth).toBe(15);
  });

  it('a customer with only carton reaches the 15-point mail-in minimum (spec 18.2 item 19, 17.3)', async () => {
    const u = await createUser(prisma, { phone: phone() });
    const carton = await createWasteItem(prisma, { unitsPerPoint: 1, pointsPerGroup: 1, dailyCapQty: 20, monthlyCapQty: 60 }); itemIds.push(carton.id);
    await createWarehouse(prisma);
    const r = await prisma.$transaction((tx) => hold.validateAndHold(tx, { path: 'CREATE', userId: u.id, method: 'MAIL_IN', lines: [{ itemId: carton.id, declaredQty: 15 }], pointId: null, now: new Date() }, { laneDropoff: true, laneMailin: true, lanePickup: false, mailinMinPoints: 15, pickupMinPoints: 10, customerMonthlyCapPoints: 60, maxOpenRequests: 3, requestHoldDays: 7 }));
    expect(r.estimatedPoints).toBe(15);
  });
});
```

  The fixture's `estimatedPoints` is `Σ floor(declaredQty / unitsPerPoint) * pointsPerGroup` with the item defaults 1 and 1 (registry 4.10), so the points sum of the first case is OPEN 3 + IN_TRANSIT 4 + COMPLETED 9 (`pointsAwarded` null, so `estimatedPoints` counts) + REVERSED 2 = 18, and 15 without the OPEN request. Run: registry 4.14 DB gate A then `cd apps/api && npx jest -c test/integration-race/jest.config.js --runInBand waste-core` Expected: PASS.

import { WasteShipLockService } from './waste-ship-lock.service';

describe('WasteShipLockService.shouldLock', () => {

    const $queryRaw = jest.fn().mockResolvedValue([{ should_lock: true }]);
    const svc = new WasteShipLockService({} as PrismaService, {} as WasteNotifierService);
    await expect(svc.shouldLock({ $queryRaw } as never, 'u1', 50, 3)).resolves.toBe(true);
