```ts
    expect(values).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(['u1', 50, 3]));
    expect(strings.join('?')).toContain('"closeReason" = \'PICKUP_NO_SHOW\'');
  });
  it.each([[[]], [[{ should_lock: null }]], [[{ should_lock: false }]]])('rows %j -> false', async (rows) => {
    const svc = new WasteShipLockService({} as PrismaService, {} as WasteNotifierService);
    await expect(svc.shouldLock({ $queryRaw: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(rows) } as never, 'u1', 50, 3)).resolves.toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('WasteShipLockService.evaluateAndLock order (spec 15.6, 10.2)', () => {
  it('writes the lock and its event inside step 1, notifies after commit, then runs step 2', async () => {
    const order: string[] = [];
    const tx = {
      $queryRaw: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue([{ should_lock: true }]),
      wasteUserFlags: { createMany: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue({ count: 1 }), updateMany: jest.fn(async () => { order.push('flags update'); return { count: 1 }; }) },
      wasteEvent: { create: jest.fn(async () => { order.push('event'); return { id: 'e1' }; }) },
      wasteRequest: { updateMany: jest.fn(async () => { order.push('cancel'); return { count: 1 }; }) },
    };
    const prisma = {
      $queryRaw: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue([{ should_lock: true }]),
      $transaction: jest.fn(async (fn: (t: typeof tx) => Promise<unknown>) => { const r = await fn(tx); order.push('tx end'); return r; }),
      wasteRequest: { findMany: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue([{ id: 'p1' }]) },
    };
    const notifier = { shipLocked: jest.fn(async () => { order.push('notify'); }) };
    const svc = new WasteShipLockService(prisma as never, notifier as never);
    await expect(svc.evaluateAndLock('u1', { shortfallLockPct: 50, shortfallLockStreak: 3 })).resolves.toMatchObject({ locked: true, cancelledRequestIds: ['p1'] });
    expect(order.indexOf('event')).toBeLessThan(order.indexOf('tx end'));
    expect(order.indexOf('tx end')).toBeLessThan(order.indexOf('notify'));
    expect(order.indexOf('notify')).toBeLessThan(order.indexOf('cancel'));

});
```

```ts
describe('WasteShipLockService (real Postgres, spec 15.6 cases)', () => {
  const CFG = { shortfallLockPct: 50, shortfallLockStreak: 3 };
  const t = (min: number) => new Date(Date.now() - (100 - min) * 60_000);
  let closedItemId: string | null = null;
  async function closed(userId: string, method: string, status: string, est: number, awarded: number | null, closeReason: string | null, closedAt: Date) {
    const wh = await createWarehouse(prisma);
    if (!closedItemId) {
      const item = await createWasteItem(prisma);
      itemIds.push(item.id);
      closedItemId = item.id;
    }
    const r = await createWasteRequest(prisma, { userId, method: method as never, status: status as never, pointId: wh.id, lines: [{ itemId: closedItemId, declaredQty: est }] });
    return prisma.wasteRequest.update({ where: { id: r.id }, data: { pointsAwarded: awarded, closeReason: closeReason as never, closedAt } });
  }
  it.each<[string, [string, string, number, number | null, string | null][], boolean]>([
    ['three empty MAIL_IN completions lock', [['MAIL_IN', 'COMPLETED', 10, 0, null], ['MAIL_IN', 'COMPLETED', 10, 0, null], ['MAIL_IN', 'COMPLETED', 10, 0, null]], true],
    ['two strikes are not enough', [['MAIL_IN', 'REJECTED', 10, null, null], ['MAIL_IN', 'REJECTED', 10, null, null]], false],
    ['latest good completion breaks the streak', [['MAIL_IN', 'REJECTED', 10, null, null], ['MAIL_IN', 'REJECTED', 10, null, null], ['MAIL_IN', 'COMPLETED', 10, 10, null]], false],
    ['awarded exactly 50% is not a strike', [['MAIL_IN', 'REJECTED', 10, null, null], ['MAIL_IN', 'REJECTED', 10, null, null], ['MAIL_IN', 'COMPLETED', 10, 5, null]], false],
    ['awarded 40% is a strike', [['MAIL_IN', 'REJECTED', 10, null, null], ['MAIL_IN', 'REJECTED', 10, null, null], ['MAIL_IN', 'COMPLETED', 10, 4, null]], true],
    ['REVERSED counts', [['PICKUP', 'REVERSED', 10, 10, null], ['MAIL_IN', 'REJECTED', 10, null, null], ['MAIL_IN', 'REJECTED', 10, null, null]], true],
    ['PICKUP_NO_SHOW counts', [['PICKUP', 'EXPIRED', 10, null, 'PICKUP_NO_SHOW'], ['MAIL_IN', 'REJECTED', 10, null, null], ['MAIL_IN', 'REJECTED', 10, null, null]], true],
    ['three COMPLETED at 35% lock', [['MAIL_IN', 'COMPLETED', 20, 7, null], ['MAIL_IN', 'COMPLETED', 20, 7, null], ['MAIL_IN', 'COMPLETED', 20, 7, null]], true],
    ['MAIL_IN closed LOST is never considered', [['MAIL_IN', 'REJECTED', 10, null, null], ['MAIL_IN', 'REJECTED', 10, null, null], ['MAIL_IN', 'EXPIRED', 10, null, 'LOST']], false],
    ['a newest PICKUP_NOT_DISPATCHED does not mask three earlier strikes', [['MAIL_IN', 'REJECTED', 10, null, null], ['MAIL_IN', 'REJECTED', 10, null, null], ['MAIL_IN', 'REJECTED', 10, null, null], ['PICKUP', 'EXPIRED', 10, null, 'PICKUP_NOT_DISPATCHED']], true],
    ['never considered: CANCELLED, MAIL_IN HOLD_EXPIRED, MAIL_IN LOST, PICKUP HOLD_EXPIRED, PICKUP_NOT_DISPATCHED, LOST_AT_WAREHOUSE, DROP_OFF', [['MAIL_IN', 'REJECTED', 10, null, null], ['MAIL_IN', 'REJECTED', 10, null, null], ['MAIL_IN', 'CANCELLED', 10, null, null], ['MAIL_IN', 'EXPIRED', 10, null, 'HOLD_EXPIRED'], ['PICKUP', 'EXPIRED', 10, null, 'PICKUP_NOT_DISPATCHED'], ['MAIL_IN', 'EXPIRED', 10, null, 'LOST_AT_WAREHOUSE'], ['DROP_OFF', 'REJECTED', 10, null, null], ['MAIL_IN', 'EXPIRED', 10, null, 'LOST'], ['PICKUP', 'EXPIRED', 10, null, 'HOLD_EXPIRED']], false],
  ])('%s', async (_n, rows, expected) => {
    const u = await createUser(prisma, { phone: phone() });
    for (const [i, [m, s, est, aw, cr]] of rows.entries()) await closed(u.id, m, s, est, aw, cr, t(i));
    await expect(shipLock.shouldLock(prisma, u.id, 50, 3)).resolves.toBe(expected);
  });

  it('two requests with the same closedAt are ordered by id (spec 15.6 ORDER BY "closedAt" DESC, id DESC)', async () => {
    const u = await createUser(prisma, { phone: phone() });
    const tie = t(0);
    const bad = await closed(u.id, 'MAIL_IN', 'REJECTED', 10, null, null, tie);
    const good = await closed(u.id, 'MAIL_IN', 'COMPLETED', 10, 10, null, tie);
    await closed(u.id, 'MAIL_IN', 'REJECTED', 10, null, null, t(1));
    await closed(u.id, 'MAIL_IN', 'REJECTED', 10, null, null, t(2));
    const [top] = await prisma.$queryRaw<{ id: string }[]>`SELECT id FROM waste_requests WHERE id IN (${bad.id}, ${good.id}) ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1`;
    await expect(shipLock.shouldLock(prisma, u.id, 50, 3)).resolves.toBe(top!.id === bad.id);
  });

  it('unlock does not relock on old strikes; three new strikes after shipUnlockedAt lock again', async () => {
    const u = await createUser(prisma, { phone: phone() });
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) await closed(u.id, 'MAIL_IN', 'REJECTED', 10, null, null, t(i));
    await prisma.wasteUserFlags.create({ data: { userId: u.id, shipUnlockedAt: new Date() } });
    await expect(shipLock.shouldLock(prisma, u.id, 50, 3)).resolves.toBe(false);
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) await closed(u.id, 'MAIL_IN', 'REJECTED', 10, null, null, new Date(Date.now() + (i + 1) * 1000));
    await expect(shipLock.shouldLock(prisma, u.id, 50, 3)).resolves.toBe(true);
  });

  it('evaluateAndLock: step 1 locks once, step 2 cancels only the unattached OPEN PICKUP, events and notify once', async () => {
    const u = await createUser(prisma, { phone: phone() });
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) await closed(u.id, 'MAIL_IN', 'REJECTED', 10, null, null, t(i));
    const wh = await createWarehouse(prisma);
    const item = await createWasteItem(prisma); itemIds.push(item.id);
    const pending = await createWasteRequest(prisma, { userId: u.id, method: 'PICKUP', status: 'OPEN', pointId: wh.id, lines: [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty: 10 }] });
    const order = await createOrder(prisma, { userId: u.id });
    const attached = await createWasteRequest(prisma, { userId: u.id, method: 'PICKUP', status: 'OPEN', pointId: wh.id, orderId: order.id, expiresAt: null, lines: [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty: 10 }] });
    const mail = await createWasteRequest(prisma, { userId: u.id, method: 'MAIL_IN', status: 'OPEN', pointId: wh.id, lines: [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty: 15 }] });
    const res = await shipLock.evaluateAndLock(u.id, CFG);
    expect(res.locked).toBe(true);
    expect(res.cancelledRequestIds).toEqual([pending.id]);
    const after = await prisma.wasteRequest.findMany({ where: { id: { in: [pending.id, attached.id, mail.id] } } });
    expect(after.find((r) => r.id === pending.id)).toMatchObject({ status: 'CANCELLED', closeReason: 'SHIP_LOCKED' });
    expect(after.find((r) => r.id === pending.id)!.closedAt).not.toBeNull();
    expect(after.find((r) => r.id === attached.id)!.status).toBe('OPEN');
    expect(after.find((r) => r.id === mail.id)!.status).toBe('OPEN');
    const events = await prisma.wasteEvent.findMany({ where: { OR: [{ requestId: pending.id }, { kind: 'SHIP_LOCKED', meta: { path: ['subjectUserId'], equals: u.id } }] } });
    expect(events.map((e) => [e.kind, e.actorRole]).sort()).toEqual([['REQUEST_CANCELLED_SHIP_LOCK', 'SYSTEM'], ['SHIP_LOCKED', 'SYSTEM']]);
    expect(events.find((e) => e.kind === 'SHIP_LOCKED')!.meta).toEqual({ subjectUserId: u.id, before: { shipLockedAt: null }, after: { shipLockedAt: res.shipLockedAt!.toISOString() } });
    expect(notifier.shipLocked).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(notifier.shipLocked).toHaveBeenCalledWith(u.id, res.shipLockedAt);
    await expect(shipLock.evaluateAndLock(u.id, CFG)).resolves.toMatchObject({ locked: false, cancelledRequestIds: [] });
    expect(notifier.shipLocked).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('two parallel evaluations write one SHIP_LOCKED event and notify once; an existing flags row (blockedAt set) is updated, not duplicated', async () => {
    notifier.shipLocked.mockClear();
    const u = await createUser(prisma, { phone: phone() });
    await prisma.wasteUserFlags.create({ data: { userId: u.id, blockedAt: new Date() } });
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) await closed(u.id, 'MAIL_IN', 'REJECTED', 10, null, null, t(i));
    const rs = await Promise.allSettled([shipLock.evaluateAndLock(u.id, CFG), shipLock.evaluateAndLock(u.id, CFG)]);
    expect(rs.every((r) => r.status === 'fulfilled')).toBe(true);
    expect(rs.filter((r) => r.status === 'fulfilled' && r.value.locked)).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(await prisma.wasteEvent.count({ where: { kind: 'SHIP_LOCKED', meta: { path: ['subjectUserId'], equals: u.id } } })).toBe(1);
    expect(notifier.shipLocked).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(await prisma.wasteUserFlags.findUnique({ where: { userId: u.id } })).toMatchObject({ blockedAt: expect.any(Date), shipLockedAt: expect.any(Date) });
  });

  it('a stale positive evaluation does not re-lock after an admin unlock (spec 15.6 Mở khoá)', async () => {
    notifier.shipLocked.mockClear();
    const u = await createUser(prisma, { phone: phone() });
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) await closed(u.id, 'MAIL_IN', 'REJECTED', 10, null, null, t(i));
    await prisma.wasteUserFlags.create({ data: { userId: u.id, shipUnlockedAt: new Date() } });
    const spy = jest.spyOn(shipLock, 'shouldLock').mockResolvedValueOnce(true);
    const res = await shipLock.evaluateAndLock(u.id, CFG);
    expect(spy).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
    spy.mockRestore();
    expect(res).toMatchObject({ locked: false, cancelledRequestIds: [] });
    expect((await prisma.wasteUserFlags.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { userId: u.id } })).shipLockedAt).toBeNull();
    expect(await prisma.wasteEvent.count({ where: { kind: 'SHIP_LOCKED', meta: { path: ['subjectUserId'], equals: u.id } } })).toBe(0);
    expect(notifier.shipLocked).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `WasteShipLockService` in `apps/api/src/modules/waste-exchange/core/waste-ship-lock.service.ts`.** Constructor `(private readonly prisma: PrismaService, private readonly notifier: WasteNotifierService)`; `logger = new Logger(WasteShipLockService.name)`.
  - `shouldLock(db, userId, pct, streak)`: the SQL of spec 15.6 verbatim through `db.$queryRaw` (tagged template), with `$1` = `${userId}`, `$2` = `${pct}::int`, `$3` = `${streak}::int` at each of its places (the `IN` lists stay literal); returns `rows[0]?.should_lock === true`.
  - `evaluateAndLock(userId, cfg)`: `shouldLock(this.prisma, userId, cfg.shortfallLockPct, cfg.shortfallLockStreak)`; false returns `{ locked: false, shipLockedAt: null, cancelledRequestIds: [] }`. Step 1, one `this.prisma.$transaction`: `wasteUserFlags.createMany({ data: [{ userId }], skipDuplicates: true })`; ``await tx.$queryRaw`SELECT 1 FROM waste_user_flags WHERE "userId" = ${userId} FOR UPDATE` ``; re-run `this.shouldLock(tx, userId, cfg.shortfallLockPct, cfg.shortfallLockStreak)` and return the not-locked result when it is false (no event, no notify, no step 2: an admin unlock that committed after the first evaluation moved `shipUnlockedAt`, spec 15.6 "Mở khoá"); `const at = new Date()`; `wasteUserFlags.updateMany({ where: { userId, shipLockedAt: null }, data: { shipLockedAt: at } })`; count 1 writes `writeWasteEvent(tx, { kind: 'SHIP_LOCKED', actorUserId: null, actorRole: 'SYSTEM', meta: { subjectUserId: userId, before: { shipLockedAt: null }, after: { shipLockedAt: at.toISOString() } } })` (registry 4.5: every event meta carries `before` and `after`); count 0 returns the not-locked result (no notify, no step 2). After commit: `await this.notifier.shipLocked(userId, at)`. Step 2: `findMany({ where: { userId, method: 'PICKUP', status: 'OPEN', orderId: null }, select: { id: true } })`, then for each row its own `$transaction`: `updateMany({ where: { id, userId, method: 'PICKUP', status: 'OPEN', orderId: null }, data: { status: 'CANCELLED', closedAt: new Date(), closeReason: 'SHIP_LOCKED' } })`, count 1 writes `REQUEST_CANCELLED_SHIP_LOCK` (`actorRole 'SYSTEM'`, `requestId`, `meta { subjectUserId, before: { status: 'OPEN' }, after: { status: 'CANCELLED', closeReason: 'SHIP_LOCKED' } }`) and pushes the id; a thrown error is logged with `logger.warn` and the loop continues (spec 15.6: step 2 failing is harmless). Returns `{ locked: true, shipLockedAt: at, cancelledRequestIds }`. Never called inside a caller's transaction (registry 4.12 item 16); no notification for the cancelled PICKUPs (the lock notice covers them, spec 15.6).
  - `expiresAt` is left as it is: `waste_requests_expires_at_chk` (M1.2) only forbids it on an attached OPEN row and on MAIL_IN IN_TRANSIT, and requires it on PICKUP IN_TRANSIT; a CANCELLED row may keep it.

```ts
const D = (s: string) => new Date(s);
const line = (o: Record<string, unknown> = {}) => ({ id: 'l1', requestId: 'r1', itemId: 'carton', itemCode: 'CARTON', itemName: 'Thùng carton', unitLabel: 'thùng', unitsPerPoint: 1, pointsPerGroup: 1, dropsPerUnit: 2, gramsPerUnit: 300, declaredQty: 10, receivedQty: 8, shortfallReason: 'WET', pointsAwarded: 8, ...o });

  id: 'r1', code: 'TR-A2C4E', userId: 'u1', method: 'MAIL_IN', status: 'COMPLETED', pointId: 'wh', orderId: null,
  carrier: 'GHTK', trackingCode: 'S123', trackingNorm: 'S123', trackingConflict: true, estimatedPoints: 10, estimatedDrops: 20,
  capDayKey: '2026-10-17', capMonthKey: '2026-10', expiresAt: null, inTransitAt: D('2026-10-18T01:00:00Z'), receivedAt: D('2026-10-20T01:00:00Z'),
  completedAt: D('2026-10-20T02:00:00Z'), closedAt: D('2026-10-20T02:00:00Z'), closeReason: null, confirmedByUserId: 's1', pointsAwarded: 8, dropsAwarded: 16,
  receivedWeightGrams: 2500, carrierCostVnd: 30000, rejectionReason: null, rejectionNote: null, reversedAt: null, reversedByUserId: null, reversalReason: null,
  reversalShortfallPoints: null, idempotencyKey: 'key-12345678', consentAt: D('2026-10-17T03:00:00Z'), consentVersion: '2026-10-17.1',
  createdAt: D('2026-10-17T03:00:00Z'), updatedAt: D('2026-10-20T02:00:00Z'),
  point: { id: 'wh', kind: 'WAREHOUSE', name: 'Kho Tubu TP.HCM', addressLine: '12 Đường A', communeName: 'Phường B', provinceName: 'TP.HCM', phone: '0281234567', openingHours: { MON: [{ open: '08:00', close: '17:00' }] } },
  order: null, lines: [line()], ...o,
}) as unknown as WasteRequestFull;

const DETAIL_KEYS = ['cancellable', 'carrier', 'closeReason', 'closedAt', 'code', 'completedAt', 'createdAt', 'dropsAwarded', 'editable', 'estimatedPoints', 'expiresAt', 'id', 'impactGrams', 'inTransitAt', 'lines', 'method', 'orderCode', 'orderId', 'point', 'pointAvailable', 'pointName', 'pointsAwarded', 'receivedAt', 'rejectionNote', 'rejectionReason', 'reversalReason', 'reversedPoints', 'status', 'trackingCode'];
const SUMMARY_KEYS = ['closeReason', 'code', 'createdAt', 'estimatedPoints', 'expiresAt', 'id', 'lines', 'method', 'orderCode', 'pointName', 'pointsAwarded', 'status'];

describe('waste request mapper (allow-list, spec 15.1)', () => {
  it('detail has exactly the allowed keys; trackingConflict true never leaks; no internal field anywhere', () => {
    const dto = toRequestDetail(row(), true);
    expect(Object.keys(dto).sort()).toEqual(DETAIL_KEYS);
    const json = JSON.stringify(dto);
    for (const k of ['trackingConflict', 'trackingNorm', 'confirmedByUserId', 'reversedByUserId', 'carrierCostVnd', 'reversalShortfallPoints', 'idempotencyKey', 'capDayKey', 'capMonthKey', 'userId', 'receivedWeightGrams', 'consentVersion']) {
      expect(json).not.toContain(`"${k}"`);
    }
    expect(Object.keys(dto.lines[0]!).sort()).toEqual(['declaredQty', 'dropsPerUnit', 'gramsPerUnit', 'itemId', 'itemName', 'pointsAwarded', 'pointsPerGroup', 'receivedQty', 'shortfallReason', 'unitLabel', 'unitsPerPoint']);
    expect(dto.point).toEqual({ id: 'wh', kind: 'WAREHOUSE', name: 'Kho Tubu TP.HCM', addressLine: '12 Đường A', communeName: 'Phường B', provinceName: 'TP.HCM', phone: '0281234567', openingHours: { MON: [{ open: '08:00', close: '17:00' }] } });
  });
  it('summary has exactly the allowed keys and three-field lines', () => {
    const dto = toRequestSummary(row());
    expect(Object.keys(dto).sort()).toEqual(SUMMARY_KEYS);
    expect(dto.lines).toEqual([{ itemName: 'Thùng carton', unitLabel: 'thùng', declaredQty: 10 }]);
    expect(dto).toMatchObject({ pointName: 'Kho Tubu TP.HCM', orderCode: null, createdAt: '2026-10-17T03:00:00.000Z', expiresAt: null });
  });
  it('impactGrams only for COMPLETED with every line weighed (spec 4.7, 5.7)', () => {
    expect(toRequestDetail(row(), true).impactGrams).toBe(2400);
    expect(toRequestDetail(row({ lines: [line(), line({ id: 'l2', itemId: 'film', gramsPerUnit: null })] }), true).impactGrams).toBeNull();
    expect(toRequestDetail(row({ status: 'REVERSED' }), true).impactGrams).toBeNull();
  });
  it('reversedPoints = pointsAwarded - reversalShortfallPoints, only when REVERSED', () => {

    expect(toRequestDetail(row(), true).reversedPoints).toBeNull();
  });
  it.each<[string, Record<string, unknown>, boolean, boolean]>([
    ['OPEN, no order, no tracking', { status: 'OPEN', trackingCode: null, carrier: null }, true, true],
    ['OPEN with tracking (IN_TRANSIT not yet) is not editable', { status: 'OPEN', trackingCode: 'S1' }, false, true],
    ['OPEN attached PICKUP', { status: 'OPEN', method: 'PICKUP', orderId: 'o1', order: { code: 'DH1' }, trackingCode: null }, false, false],
    ['IN_TRANSIT', { status: 'IN_TRANSIT' }, false, false],
  ])('editable/cancellable: %s', (_n, o, editable, cancellable) => {
    expect(toRequestDetail(row(o), true)).toMatchObject({ editable, cancellable });
  });
  it('closeReason and pointAvailable pass through (cancel cause: SHIP_LOCKED, ORDER_CANCELLED, null = customer)', () => {
    expect(toRequestDetail(row({ status: 'CANCELLED', closeReason: 'SHIP_LOCKED' }), false)).toMatchObject({ closeReason: 'SHIP_LOCKED', pointAvailable: false });
  });
});
```

```ts
import { NotFoundException } from '@nestjs/common';
import type { PrismaService } from '../../../prisma/prisma.service';
import { WasteRequestsService } from './waste-requests.service';
import * as mapper from './waste-request.mapper';
import { WASTE_REQUEST_FULL_INCLUDE, WASTE_REQUEST_SUMMARY_INCLUDE } from './waste-request.mapper';

const make = (found: unknown) => {
  const findFirst = jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(found);
  const findMany = jest.fn().mockResolvedValue([]);
  const access = { availablePointIds: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(new Set(['wh'])) };
  const prisma = { wasteRequest: { findFirst, findMany } } as unknown as PrismaService;
  return { svc: new WasteRequestsService(prisma, access as never, {} as never, {} as never), findFirst, findMany, access };
};

describe('WasteRequestsService reads (ownership, spec 15.1)', () => {
  it('detail looks up by id AND userId; another user\'s id is the same 404 as a missing id', async () => {
    const { svc, findFirst } = make(null);
    await expect(svc.detail('uB', 'rA')).rejects.toThrow(new NotFoundException('Không tìm thấy yêu cầu'));
    expect(findFirst).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ where: { id: 'rA', userId: 'uB' }, include: WASTE_REQUEST_FULL_INCLUDE });
  });
  it('byOrder looks up by orderId AND userId', async () => {
    const { svc, findFirst } = make(null);
    await expect(svc.byOrder('uB', 'oA')).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(NotFoundException);
    expect(findFirst).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ where: { orderId: 'oA', userId: 'uB' }, include: WASTE_REQUEST_FULL_INCLUDE });
  });
  it('list: own rows, newest first, at most 100', async () => {
    const { svc, findMany } = make(null);
    await expect(svc.list('u1')).resolves.toEqual([]);
    expect(findMany).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ where: { userId: 'u1' }, include: WASTE_REQUEST_SUMMARY_INCLUDE, orderBy: { createdAt: 'desc' }, take: 100 });
  });
  it('detail passes pointAvailable = availablePointIds([row.pointId]).has(row.pointId) to the mapper', async () => {
    const row = { id: 'r1', pointId: 'p9' };
    const { svc, access } = make(row);
    const spy = jest.spyOn(mapper, 'toRequestDetail').mockReturnValue({ id: 'r1' } as never);
    await expect(svc.detail('u1', 'r1')).resolves.toEqual({ id: 'r1' });
    expect(access.availablePointIds).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.anything(), ['p9']);
    expect(spy).toHaveBeenCalledWith(row, false);
    spy.mockRestore();
  });
});
```

```ts
jest.mock('../../staff/attendance/zalo-location', () => ({ resolveZaloLocation: jest.fn() }));
import { resolveZaloLocation } from '../../staff/attendance/zalo-location';
import { WasteCatalogueService, haversineKm, isHiddenInactive } from './waste-catalogue.service';

const NOW = new Date('2026-10-17T03:00:00.000Z');
const day = (n: number) => new Date(NOW.getTime() - n * 86_400_000);
const pt = (o: Record<string, unknown>) => ({ kind: 'PARTNER', name: 'P', addressLine: 'a', provinceCode: '79', provinceName: 'TP.HCM', communeCode: 'c', communeName: 'C', lat: null, lng: null, phone: null, openingHours: {}, status: 'ACTIVE', monthlyQuotaPoints: 100, perkText: null, perkPendingText: 'chờ duyệt', sortOrder: 0, activatedAt: day(1), lastConfirmedAt: null, items: [], ...o });
function setup(points: unknown[], o: { available?: string[]; usage?: { pointId: string; pointsUsed: number }[] } = {}) {
  const prisma = {
    wastePoint: { findMany: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(points) },
    wasteItem: { findMany: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue([{ id: 'carton' }, { id: 'film' }]) },
    wastePointUsage: { findMany: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(o.usage ?? []) },
  };
  const access = { availablePointIds: jest.fn(async (_d: unknown, ids: string[]) => new Set(o.available ?? ids)) };
  const config = { snapshot: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue({ pointInactiveDays: 60 }) };
  const svc = new WasteCatalogueService(prisma as never, access as never, config as never);
  jest.useFakeTimers({ now: NOW });
  return { svc, prisma };
}
afterEach(() => jest.useRealTimers());

describe('isHiddenInactive (spec 5.5)', () => {
  it.each<[string, Record<string, unknown>, boolean]>([
    ['warehouse never', { kind: 'WAREHOUSE', lastConfirmedAt: day(400), activatedAt: day(400) }, false],
    ['later of the two dates counts', { kind: 'PARTNER', lastConfirmedAt: day(10), activatedAt: day(200) }, false],
    ['61 days idle', { kind: 'PARTNER', lastConfirmedAt: day(61), activatedAt: day(90) }, true],
    ['exactly 60 days is still visible', { kind: 'PARTNER', lastConfirmedAt: null, activatedAt: day(60) }, false],
    ['no dates at all is visible', { kind: 'PARTNER', lastConfirmedAt: null, activatedAt: null }, false],
  ])('%s', (_n, p, hidden) => expect(isHiddenInactive(p as never, NOW, 60)).toBe(hidden));
});

describe('WasteCatalogueService.listPoints', () => {
  it('available and not hidden, quotaFull derived, warehouse accepts every ACTIVE item, PARTNER only its links; pending perk never exposed', async () => {
    const { svc, prisma } = setup([
      pt({ id: 'wh', kind: 'WAREHOUSE', monthlyQuotaPoints: null, activatedAt: day(500) }),
      pt({ id: 'p1', items: [{ itemId: 'carton' }], perkText: 'Giảm 5% khi mua tại quầy' }),
      pt({ id: 'p2' }),
      pt({ id: 'idle', activatedAt: day(90) }),
      pt({ id: 'noOwner' }),
    ], { available: ['wh', 'p1', 'p2', 'idle'], usage: [{ pointId: 'p2', pointsUsed: 100 }] });
    const list = await svc.listPoints('79');
    expect(list.map((p) => [p.id, p.acceptedItemIds, p.quotaFull, p.distanceKm])).toEqual([
      ['wh', ['carton', 'film'], false, null], ['p1', ['carton'], false, null], ['p2', [], true, null],
    ]);
    expect(list[1]!.perkText).toBe('Giảm 5% khi mua tại quầy');
    expect(JSON.stringify(list)).not.toContain('perkPendingText');
    expect(Object.keys(list[1]!).sort()).toEqual(['acceptedItemIds', 'addressLine', 'communeCode', 'communeName', 'distanceKm', 'id', 'kind', 'lat', 'lng', 'name', 'openingHours', 'perkText', 'phone', 'provinceCode', 'provinceName', 'quotaFull']);
    expect(prisma.wastePoint.findMany).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.objectContaining({ where: { status: 'ACTIVE', provinceCode: '79' } }));
    expect(prisma.wastePointUsage.findMany).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ where: { pointId: { in: ['wh', 'p1', 'p2'] }, monthKey: '2026-10' }, select: { pointId: true, pointsUsed: true } });
  });
  it('returns at most 200 points', async () => {
    const { svc } = setup(Array.from({ length: 205 }, (_, i) => pt({ id: `p${i}` })));
    expect(await svc.listPoints()).toHaveLength(200);
  });
});

describe('WasteCatalogueService.nearbyPoints (spec 15.2, 14.4)', () => {
  const pts = [pt({ id: 'far', lat: 10.9, lng: 106.7 }), pt({ id: 'near', lat: 10.78, lng: 106.7 }), pt({ id: 'nolatlng' })];
  it('sorts by distance, points without coordinates last, distance rounded to 0.1 km', async () => {
    (resolveZaloLocation as jest.Mock).mockResolvedValue({ lat: 10.77, lng: 106.7 });
    const { svc } = setup(pts);
    const list = await svc.nearbyPoints({ locationToken: 'loc', zaloAccessToken: 'acc' });
    expect(list.map((p) => p.id)).toEqual(['near', 'far', 'nolatlng']);
    expect(list[0]!.distanceKm).toBe(1.1);
    expect(list[2]!.distanceKm).toBeNull();
  });
  it.each([['null location', () => Promise.resolve(null)], ['throws', () => Promise.reject(new Error('zalo down'))]])('%s -> same list without distances', async (_n, impl) => {
    (resolveZaloLocation as jest.Mock).mockImplementation(impl);
    const { svc } = setup(pts);
    expect((await svc.nearbyPoints({ locationToken: 'loc', zaloAccessToken: 'acc' })).map((p) => [p.id, p.distanceKm])).toEqual([['far', null], ['near', null], ['nolatlng', null]]);
  });
  it('haversineKm', () => expect(Math.round(haversineKm({ lat: 10.77, lng: 106.7 }, { lat: 10.78, lng: 106.7 }) * 1000)).toBe(1112));
});

describe('WasteCatalogueService.listItems', () => {
  it('ACTIVE and COMING_SOON only, by sortOrder then name; exactly the 15 DTO fields (spec 15.2)', async () => {
    const { svc, prisma } = setup([]);
    prisma.wasteItem.findMany.mockResolvedValueOnce([{
      id: 'carton', code: 'CARTON', name: 'Thùng carton', unitLabel: 'thùng', unitsPerPoint: 1, pointsPerGroup: 1, dropsPerUnit: 2, gramsPerUnit: 300,
      dailyCapQty: 20, monthlyCapQty: 60, status: 'ACTIVE', prepInstructions: null, imageUrl: null, hazardous: false, sortOrder: 1,
      createdAt: new Date('2026-10-01T00:00:00Z'), updatedAt: new Date('2026-10-02T00:00:00Z'),
    }]);
    const out = await svc.listItems();
    expect(prisma.wasteItem.findMany).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ where: { status: { in: ['ACTIVE', 'COMING_SOON'] } }, orderBy: [{ sortOrder: 'asc' }, { name: 'asc' }] });
    expect(Object.keys(out[0]!).sort()).toEqual(['code', 'dailyCapQty', 'dropsPerUnit', 'gramsPerUnit', 'hazardous', 'id', 'imageUrl', 'monthlyCapQty', 'name', 'pointsPerGroup', 'prepInstructions', 'sortOrder', 'status', 'unitLabel', 'unitsPerPoint']);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { WasteMeService } from './waste-me.service';

const SNAP = { enabled: true, mailinMinPoints: 15, pickupMinPoints: 10, maxOpenRequests: 3, customerMonthlyCapPoints: 60, mailinMaxWeightKg: 20, mailinMaxEdgeCm: 50, rulesText: '' };
const ITEM = { id: 'carton', code: 'CARTON', name: 'Thùng carton', unitLabel: 'thùng', unitsPerPoint: 1, pointsPerGroup: 1, dropsPerUnit: 2, dailyCapQty: 20, monthlyCapQty: 60, status: 'ACTIVE', prepInstructions: null, imageUrl: null, gramsPerUnit: 300, hazardous: false, sortOrder: 1 };

    user: { findUnique: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(user) },
    wasteItem: { findMany: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue([ITEM]) },

  };

    config: { snapshot: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue({ ...SNAP, ...o.snap }) },
    systemConfig: { get: jest.fn(async (k: string, d: unknown) => ({ 'waste.enabled': true, 'waste.lane_dropoff_enabled': true, 'waste.lane_mailin_enabled': true, 'waste.lane_pickup_enabled': false } as Record<string, unknown>)[k] ?? d) },
    access: { listOperablePoints: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(o.desk ?? WH_DESK) },
    hold: { capUsage: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue({ itemDay: new Map([['carton', 5]]), itemMonth: new Map([['carton', 12]]), pointsMonth: 12, openCount: 1, pickupPendingCode: null }) },

}

});
```

```ts
import { ForbiddenException } from '@nestjs/common';
import { HTTP_CODE_METADATA, METHOD_METADATA, PATH_METADATA } from '@nestjs/common/constants';
import { Test } from '@nestjs/testing';
import { THROTTLER_LIMIT, THROTTLER_TTL } from '@nestjs/throttler/dist/throttler.constants';
import { createValidationPipe } from '../../../common/validation-pipe';
import { PrismaService } from '../../../prisma/prisma.service';
import { WasteAccessService } from '../core/waste-access.service';
import { WasteConfigService } from '../core/waste-config.service';
import { WasteCatalogueService } from './waste-catalogue.service';
import { WasteCustomerController, WasteNearbyDto, WastePointsQueryDto } from './waste-customer.controller';
import { WasteMeService } from './waste-me.service';
import { WasteRequestsService } from './waste-requests.service';

const OFF = 'Tính năng Đổi rác lấy quà đang tắt. Liên hệ quản trị viên.';
const users: Record<string, unknown> = {
  real: { id: 'real', phone: '0901234567', zaloId: 'z1', isBlocked: false, mergedIntoId: null, wasteFlags: null },
  guest: { id: 'guest', phone: null, zaloId: 'guest_d', isBlocked: false, mergedIntoId: null, wasteFlags: null },
  blocked: { id: 'blocked', phone: '0901234568', zaloId: 'z2', isBlocked: false, mergedIntoId: null, wasteFlags: { blockedAt: new Date(), shipLockedAt: null } },
};
const svc = <T extends string>(...m: T[]) => Object.fromEntries(m.map((k) => [k, jest.fn().mockResolvedValue({ ok: k })])) as Record<T, jest.Mock>;
async function build(enabled: boolean) {
  const me = svc('me', 'rules');
  const catalogue = svc('listItems', 'listPoints', 'nearbyPoints');
  const requests = svc('list', 'detail', 'byOrder');
  const mod = await Test.createTestingModule({
    controllers: [WasteCustomerController],
    providers: [
      WasteAccessService,
      { provide: PrismaService, useValue: { user: { findUnique: jest.fn(async ({ where }: { where: { id: string } }) => users[where.id] ?? null) } } },
      { provide: WasteConfigService, useValue: { isEnabled: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(enabled) } },
      { provide: WasteMeService, useValue: me },
      { provide: WasteCatalogueService, useValue: catalogue },
      { provide: WasteRequestsService, useValue: requests },
    ],
  }).compile();
  return { c: mod.get(WasteCustomerController), me, catalogue, requests };
}

describe('WasteCustomerController read routes (spec 15.1)', () => {
  it('/me and /rules need neither a real user nor the programme flag', async () => {
    const { c, me } = await build(false);
    await expect(c.me('guest')).resolves.toEqual({ ok: 'me' });
    await expect(c.rules()).resolves.toEqual({ ok: 'rules' });
    expect(me.me).toHaveBeenCalledWith('guest');
  });
  it('items, points, nearby are FULL: programme off, guest, blocked -> 403, service not called', async () => {
    const off = await build(false);
    await expect(off.c.items('real')).rejects.toThrow(new ForbiddenException(OFF));
    const on = await build(true);
    await expect(on.c.points('guest', {})).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ForbiddenException);
    await expect(on.c.nearby('blocked', { locationToken: 't', zaloAccessToken: 'a' })).rejects.toThrow('Tài khoản của bạn tạm không tham gia Đổi rác, liên hệ Tubu Tree.');
    expect(on.catalogue.listPoints).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    await expect(on.c.points('real', { provinceCode: '79' })).resolves.toEqual({ ok: 'listPoints' });
    expect(on.catalogue.listPoints).toHaveBeenCalledWith('79');
  });
  it('own reads are OWN: work with the programme off and for a blocked customer; guest 403', async () => {
    const { c, requests } = await build(false);
    await expect(c.list('blocked')).resolves.toEqual({ ok: 'list' });
    await expect(c.detail('blocked', 'r1')).resolves.toEqual({ ok: 'detail' });
    await expect(c.byOrder('real', 'o1')).resolves.toEqual({ ok: 'byOrder' });
    expect(requests.detail).toHaveBeenCalledWith('blocked', 'r1');
    expect(requests.byOrder).toHaveBeenCalledWith('real', 'o1');
    await expect(c.list('guest')).rejects.toThrow('Ưu đãi này chỉ dành cho tài khoản đã đăng nhập Zalo. Vui lòng đăng nhập bằng Zalo rồi thử lại.');
  });
  it('route table: paths, by-order declared before :id, nearby POST 200 with 30/min', () => {
    const p = WasteCustomerController.prototype;
    expect(Reflect.getMetadata(PATH_METADATA, WasteCustomerController)).toBe('waste');
    const routes = Object.getOwnPropertyNames(p).filter((k) => Reflect.getMetadata(METHOD_METADATA, (p as never)[k]) !== undefined).map((k) => [k, Reflect.getMetadata(METHOD_METADATA, (p as never)[k]), Reflect.getMetadata(PATH_METADATA, (p as never)[k])]);
    expect(routes).toEqual([
      ['me', 0, 'me'], ['rules', 0, 'rules'], ['items', 0, 'items'], ['points', 0, 'points'], ['nearby', 1, 'points/nearby'],
      ['list', 0, 'requests'], ['byOrder', 0, 'requests/by-order/:orderId'], ['detail', 0, 'requests/:id'],
    ]);
    expect(Reflect.getMetadata(HTTP_CODE_METADATA, p.nearby)).toBe(200);
    expect(Reflect.getMetadata(`${THROTTLER_LIMIT}default`, p.nearby)).toBe(30);
    expect(Reflect.getMetadata(`${THROTTLER_TTL}default`, p.nearby)).toBe(60_000);
  });
});

describe('read DTOs (global ValidationPipe)', () => {
  const pipe = createValidationPipe();
  const run = (metatype: unknown, value: unknown, type: 'body' | 'query' = 'body') => pipe.transform(value, { type, metatype: metatype as never, data: '' });
  it('nearby: tokens only in the body, both required, bounded; extra fields rejected', async () => {
    await expect(run(WasteNearbyDto, { locationToken: 't', zaloAccessToken: 'a', provinceCode: '79' })).resolves.toBeInstanceOf(WasteNearbyDto);
    await expect(run(WasteNearbyDto, { zaloAccessToken: 'a' })).rejects.toThrow();
    await expect(run(WasteNearbyDto, { locationToken: 'x'.repeat(2049), zaloAccessToken: 'a' })).rejects.toThrow();
    await expect(run(WasteNearbyDto, { locationToken: 't', zaloAccessToken: 'x'.repeat(4097) })).rejects.toThrow();
    await expect(run(WasteNearbyDto, { locationToken: 't', zaloAccessToken: 'a', lat: 10 })).rejects.toThrow();
  });
  it('points query: optional provinceCode, at most 20 chars; no token field accepted', async () => {
    await expect(run(WastePointsQueryDto, {}, 'query')).resolves.toBeInstanceOf(WastePointsQueryDto);
    await expect(run(WastePointsQueryDto, { provinceCode: 'x'.repeat(21) }, 'query')).rejects.toThrow();
    await expect(run(WastePointsQueryDto, { locationToken: 't' }, 'query')).rejects.toThrow();
  });
});
```

```ts
    const bare = setup({ blocks: [{ code: 'SHIP_LOCKED' }] });
    expect((await bare.svc.preview('u1', body)).blocks).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

```ts
const SNAP = { enabled: true, laneDropoff: true, laneMailin: true, lanePickup: false, mailinMinPoints: 15, pickupMinPoints: 10, customerMonthlyCapPoints: 60, maxOpenRequests: 3, requestHoldDays: 7 };
type CreatePrior = { id: string; method?: string; pointId?: string; lines?: { itemId: string; declaredQty: number }[] } | null;
function makeCreate(o: { prior: CreatePrior; hold?: unknown; failWith?: unknown[]; reread?: CreatePrior; codeTaken?: number; snap?: Partial<typeof SNAP> }) {
  const order: string[] = [];
  const fails = [...(o.failWith ?? [])];
  let taken = o.codeTaken ?? 0;
  const tx = {
    $executeRaw: jest.fn(async (..._sql: unknown[]) => { order.push('lock'); return 1; }),
    wasteRequest: {
      findUnique: jest.fn(async (args: { where: { code?: string; userId_idempotencyKey?: unknown } }) => {
        if (args.where.code === undefined) { order.push('replay'); return o.prior; }
        if (!order.includes('codeCheck')) order.push('codeCheck');
        if (taken > 0) { taken -= 1; return { id: 'taken' }; }
        return null;
      }),
      create: jest.fn(async (_args: { data: Record<string, unknown> }) => { order.push('create'); return { id: 'r1' }; }),
    },
    wasteEvent: { create: jest.fn(async (_args: { data: Record<string, unknown> }) => { order.push('event'); return { id: 'e1' }; }) },
  };
  const prisma = {
    $transaction: jest.fn(async (fn: (t: typeof tx) => Promise<unknown>) => {
      const f = fails.shift();
      if (f !== undefined) throw f;
      return fn(tx);
    }),
    wasteRequest: { findUnique: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(o.reread ?? null) },
  };
  const hold = { validateAndHold: jest.fn(async (_tx: unknown, _input: unknown, _cfg: unknown) => { order.push('hold'); return o.hold; }) };
  const config = { snapshot: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue({ ...SNAP, ...o.snap }) };
  const svc = new WasteRequestsService(prisma as never, {} as never, hold as never, config as never, {} as never);
  const loadDetail = jest.spyOn(svc, 'loadDetail').mockImplementation(async (...args: unknown[]) => ({ id: args[2] }) as never);
  return { svc, tx, prisma, hold, loadDetail, order };
}

describe('WasteRequestsService.create (spec 11.2, 15.2)', () => {
  const HOLD = { pointId: 'p1', capDayKey: '2026-10-17', capMonthKey: '2026-10', expiresAt: new Date('2026-10-24T03:00:00Z'), lines: [{ itemId: 'carton', itemCode: 'CARTON', itemName: 'Thùng carton', unitLabel: 'thùng', unitsPerPoint: 1, pointsPerGroup: 1, dropsPerUnit: 2, gramsPerUnit: 300, declaredQty: 5, hazardous: false }], estimatedPoints: 5, estimatedDrops: 10 };
  const body = { method: 'DROP_OFF' as const, pointId: 'p1', lines: [{ itemId: 'carton', declaredQty: 5 }], consent: true as const };
  beforeEach(() => jest.mocked(computeWastePublicFlags).mockClear());
  it('takes the customer lock first, then the replay read, then validateAndHold; writes one OPEN request and one CUSTOMER event', async () => {
    const { svc, tx, hold, order } = makeCreate({ prior: null, hold: HOLD });
    await svc.create('u1', 'key-00000001', body);
    expect(order).toEqual(['lock', 'replay', 'hold', 'codeCheck', 'create', 'event']);
    expect(tx.wasteRequest.create.mock.calls[0]![0].data).toMatchObject({ userId: 'u1', method: 'DROP_OFF', status: 'OPEN', pointId: 'p1', estimatedPoints: 5, estimatedDrops: 10, capDayKey: '2026-10-17', capMonthKey: '2026-10', expiresAt: HOLD.expiresAt, idempotencyKey: 'key-00000001', consentVersion: '2026-10-17.1' });
    expect(tx.wasteRequest.create.mock.calls[0]![0].data.code).toMatch(/^TR-[2-9A-HJKMNP-TV-Z]{5}$/);
    expect(tx.wasteEvent.create.mock.calls[0]![0].data).toMatchObject({ kind: 'REQUEST_CREATED', actorUserId: 'u1', actorRole: 'CUSTOMER', pointId: 'p1', meta: { before: null, after: { method: 'DROP_OFF', pointId: 'p1', estimatedPoints: 5, lines: body.lines } } });
    expect(hold.validateAndHold.mock.calls[0]![1]).toMatchObject({ path: 'CREATE', userId: 'u1', method: 'DROP_OFF', pointId: 'p1' });
  });
  it('same key + same content returns the stored request without any check (even at max open)', async () => {
    const { svc, tx, hold, loadDetail } = makeCreate({ prior: { id: 'r0', method: 'DROP_OFF', pointId: 'p1', lines: [{ itemId: 'carton', declaredQty: 5 }] } });
    await svc.create('u1', 'key-00000001', body);
    expect(hold.validateAndHold).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(tx.wasteRequest.create).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(tx.wasteEvent.create).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(loadDetail).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.anything(), 'u1', 'r0');
  });
  it.each<[string, Record<string, unknown>, Record<string, unknown>, boolean]>([
    ['MAIL_IN ignores pointId', { method: 'MAIL_IN', pointId: 'wh' }, { method: 'MAIL_IN', pointId: 'anything', packagingConsent: true, postagePaidConsent: true }, true],
    ['PICKUP ignores pointId', { method: 'PICKUP', pointId: 'wh' }, { method: 'PICKUP', pointId: undefined }, true],
    ['DROP_OFF compares pointId', { method: 'DROP_OFF', pointId: 'p1' }, { method: 'DROP_OFF', pointId: 'p2' }, false],
    ['different qty', { method: 'DROP_OFF', pointId: 'p1', lines: [{ itemId: 'carton', declaredQty: 6 }] }, {}, false],
    ['different method', { method: 'MAIL_IN', pointId: 'wh' }, {}, false],
    ['line order does not matter', { method: 'DROP_OFF', pointId: 'p1', lines: [{ itemId: 'film', declaredQty: 1 }, { itemId: 'carton', declaredQty: 5 }] }, { lines: [{ itemId: 'carton', declaredQty: 5 }, { itemId: 'film', declaredQty: 1 }] }, true],
  ])('replay rule: %s', async (_n, prior, over, same) => {
    const { svc } = makeCreate({ prior: { id: 'r0', lines: body.lines, ...prior } });
    const call = svc.create('u1', 'key-00000001', { ...body, ...over } as never);
    if (same) {
      await expect(call).resolves.toBeDefined();
    } else {
      await expect(call).rejects.toThrow(new ConflictException('Mã chống gửi lặp này đã dùng cho một yêu cầu khác'));
      await expect(call).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ConflictException);
    }
  });
  it('MAIL_IN without both commitments -> 400 after the replay check, nothing held', async () => {
    const { svc, hold } = makeCreate({ prior: null });
    const call = svc.create('u1', 'key-00000001', { ...body, method: 'MAIL_IN', packagingConsent: true });
    await expect(call).rejects.toThrow(new BadRequestException('Bạn cần đồng ý quy tắc đóng gói và cam kết đã trả đủ cước để gửi về kho'));
    await expect(call).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(BadRequestException);
    expect(hold.validateAndHold).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
  it('MAIL_IN passes pointId null to validateAndHold', async () => {
    const { svc, hold } = makeCreate({ prior: null, hold: { ...HOLD, pointId: 'wh' } });
    await svc.create('u1', 'key-00000001', { ...body, method: 'MAIL_IN', pointId: 'p9', packagingConsent: true, postagePaidConsent: true });
    expect(hold.validateAndHold.mock.calls[0]![1]).toMatchObject({ method: 'MAIL_IN', pointId: null });
  });
  it('PICKUP while Gomdon is off passes lanePickup false to validateAndHold (spec 11.5)', async () => {
    const { svc, hold } = makeCreate({ prior: null, hold: { ...HOLD, pointId: 'wh' }, snap: { lanePickup: true } });
    jest.mocked(computeWastePublicFlags).mockResolvedValueOnce({ enabled: true, dropoff: true, mailin: true, pickup: false });
    await svc.create('u1', 'key-00000001', { method: 'PICKUP', lines: [{ itemId: 'carton', declaredQty: 10 }], consent: true });
    expect(hold.validateAndHold.mock.calls[0]![2]).toMatchObject({ lanePickup: false });
  });
  it('DROP_OFF never reads the public flags', async () => {
    const { svc } = makeCreate({ prior: null, hold: HOLD });
    await svc.create('u1', 'key-00000001', body);
    expect(computeWastePublicFlags).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
  it.each([
    ['a plain error carrying code and meta', Object.assign(new Error('dup'), { code: 'P2002', meta: { target: ['userId', 'idempotencyKey'] } })],
    ['a Prisma known request error', new Prisma.PrismaClientKnownRequestError('dup', { code: 'P2002', clientVersion: 'test', meta: { target: ['userId', 'idempotencyKey'] } })],
  ])('a P2002 on (userId, idempotencyKey) escaping the tx (%s) is re-read outside and replayed', async (_n, dup) => {
    const { svc, loadDetail, prisma } = makeCreate({ prior: null, hold: HOLD, failWith: [dup], reread: { id: 'r7', method: 'DROP_OFF', pointId: 'p1', lines: body.lines } });
    await svc.create('u1', 'key-00000001', body);
    expect(loadDetail).toHaveBeenCalledWith(prisma, 'u1', 'r7');
    expect(prisma.$transaction).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });
  it('a P2002 on code re-runs the whole transaction once; a second one is rethrown (registry 4.3)', async () => {
    const dupCode = () => Object.assign(new Error('dup'), { code: 'P2002', meta: { target: ['code'] } });
    const once = makeCreate({ prior: null, hold: HOLD, failWith: [dupCode()] });
    await once.svc.create('u1', 'key-00000001', body);
    expect(once.prisma.$transaction).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
    expect(once.loadDetail).toHaveBeenCalledWith(once.prisma, 'u1', 'r1');
    const twice = makeCreate({ prior: null, hold: HOLD, failWith: [dupCode(), dupCode()] });
    await expect(twice.svc.create('u1', 'key-00000001', body)).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'P2002' });
    expect(twice.prisma.$transaction).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
  });
  it('a TR code taken at the pre-insert check is regenerated (max 5 tries)', async () => {
    const { svc, tx } = makeCreate({ prior: null, hold: HOLD, codeTaken: 2 });
    await svc.create('u1', 'key-00000001', body);
    expect(tx.wasteRequest.findUnique.mock.calls.filter((c) => c[0].where.code !== undefined).length).toBe(3);
  });
  it('five taken TR codes -> 409 Vui lòng thử lại, nothing created', async () => {
    const { svc, tx } = makeCreate({ prior: null, hold: HOLD, codeTaken: 5 });
    const call = svc.create('u1', 'key-00000001', body);
    await expect(call).rejects.toThrow(new ConflictException('Vui lòng thử lại'));
    await expect(call).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ConflictException);
    expect(tx.wasteRequest.create).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});
```

```ts
describe('WasteCustomerController.create (spec 11.2, 15.1)', () => {
  const body = { method: 'DROP_OFF' as const, pointId: 'p1', lines: [{ itemId: 'carton', declaredQty: 5 }], consent: true as const };
  const invalidKey = (() => { try { parseTreeBuyKey('bad'); } catch (e) { return (e as Error).message; } return ''; })();
  it('FULL gate runs before the key: guest, no phone, waste-blocked and programme off are 403; the service is not called', async () => {
    const on = await build(true);
    await expect(on.c.create('guest', undefined, body)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ForbiddenException);
    await expect(on.c.create('nophone', 'key-00000001', body)).rejects.toThrow(new ForbiddenException('Bạn cần chia sẻ số điện thoại để dùng Đổi rác lấy quà.'));
    await expect(on.c.create('blocked', 'key-00000001', body)).rejects.toThrow(new ForbiddenException('Tài khoản của bạn tạm không tham gia Đổi rác, liên hệ Tubu Tree.'));
    const off = await build(false);
    await expect(off.c.create('real', 'key-00000001', body)).rejects.toThrow(new ForbiddenException(OFF));
    expect(on.requests.create).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(off.requests.create).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
  it('Idempotency-Key: missing or malformed is 400; a valid key reaches the service with the body', async () => {
    const { c, requests } = await build(true);
    await expect(c.create('real', undefined, body)).rejects.toThrow(new BadRequestException('Thiếu Idempotency-Key: cần 8 đến 128 ký tự gồm chữ cái, chữ số, dấu gạch dưới (_) hoặc gạch nối (-).'));
    await expect(c.create('real', 'short', body)).rejects.toThrow(new BadRequestException(invalidKey));
    expect(requests.create).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    await expect(c.create('real', 'key-00000001', body)).resolves.toEqual({ ok: 'create' });
    expect(requests.create).toHaveBeenCalledWith('real', 'key-00000001', body);
  });
  it('POST requests with 10/min', () => {
    const p = WasteCustomerController.prototype;
    expect(Reflect.getMetadata(`${THROTTLER_LIMIT}default`, p.create)).toBe(10);
    expect(Reflect.getMetadata(`${THROTTLER_TTL}default`, p.create)).toBe(60_000);
  });
});
```

```ts
describe('waste create races (real Postgres, spec 17.3)', () => {
  const free = { dailyCapQty: null, monthlyCapQty: null };
  const openFixture = (u: { id: string }, item: { id: string }, point: { id: string }) =>
    createWasteRequest(prisma, { userId: u.id, method: 'DROP_OFF', status: 'OPEN', pointId: point.id, lines: [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty: 1 }] });
  for (const [label, first] of [['A then B', 'A'], ['B then A', 'B']] as const) {
    const race = (u: { id: string }, a: () => Promise<unknown>, b: () => Promise<unknown>) =>
      first === 'A' ? raceWithHold(prisma, customerLock(u), a, b) : raceWithHold(prisma, customerLock(u), b, a);
    it(`day cap: create x create (${label}) -> one succeeds, total within 10`, async () => {
      const { u, item, point } = await scene({ dailyCapQty: 10, monthlyCapQty: null });
      const lines = [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty: 6 }];
      const rs = await race(u, () => create(u, randKey(), lines, 'DROP_OFF', point.id), () => create(u, randKey(), lines, 'DROP_OFF', point.id));
      expect(errorsOf(rs).join()).not.toMatch(/40P01|P2034/);
      expect(rs.map((r) => r.status)).toEqual(['fulfilled', 'rejected']);
      expect(reasonOf(rs[1])).toContain('hôm nay bạn chỉ còn mang tới điểm nhận được 4');
      expect(await prisma.wasteRequest.count({ where: { userId: u.id } })).toBe(1);
    });
    it(`month cap: create x create (${label})`, async () => {
      const { u, item, point } = await scene({ dailyCapQty: null, monthlyCapQty: 10 });
      const lines = [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty: 6 }];
      const rs = await race(u, () => create(u, randKey(), lines, 'DROP_OFF', point.id), () => create(u, randKey(), lines, 'DROP_OFF', point.id));
      expect(errorsOf(rs).join()).not.toMatch(/40P01|P2034/);
      expect(rs.map((r) => r.status)).toEqual(['fulfilled', 'rejected']);
      expect(reasonOf(rs[1])).toContain('tháng này bạn chỉ còn gửi được 4');
      expect(await prisma.wasteRequest.count({ where: { userId: u.id } })).toBe(1);
    });
    it(`points cap: create x create (${label})`, async () => {
      const { u, item, point } = await scene(free);
      const lines = [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty: 40 }];
      const rs = await race(u, () => create(u, randKey(), lines, 'DROP_OFF', point.id), () => create(u, randKey(), lines, 'DROP_OFF', point.id));
      expect(errorsOf(rs).join()).not.toMatch(/40P01|P2034/);
      expect(rs.map((r) => r.status)).toEqual(['fulfilled', 'rejected']);
      expect(reasonOf(rs[1])).toBe('Tháng này bạn chỉ còn 20 Điểm Xanh trong trần đổi rác');
      expect(await prisma.wasteRequest.count({ where: { userId: u.id } })).toBe(1);
    });
    it(`open cap: create x create at 2 open (${label})`, async () => {
      const { u, item, point } = await scene(free);
      await openFixture(u, item, point);
      await openFixture(u, item, point);
      const lines = [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty: 1 }];
      const rs = await race(u, () => create(u, randKey(), lines, 'DROP_OFF', point.id), () => create(u, randKey(), lines, 'DROP_OFF', point.id));
      expect(errorsOf(rs).join()).not.toMatch(/40P01|P2034/);
      expect(rs.map((r) => r.status)).toEqual(['fulfilled', 'rejected']);
      expect(reasonOf(rs[1])).toBe('Bạn đang có 3 yêu cầu chưa xong, hãy hoàn tất hoặc huỷ bớt trước khi tạo thêm');
      expect(await prisma.wasteRequest.count({ where: { userId: u.id, status: 'OPEN' } })).toBe(3);
    });
    it(`PICKUP create x create (${label}) -> one row`, async () => {
      const { u, item } = await scene(free);
      const lines = [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty: 10 }];
      const rs = await race(u, () => create(u, randKey(), lines, 'PICKUP'), () => create(u, randKey(), lines, 'PICKUP'));
      expect(errorsOf(rs).join()).not.toMatch(/40P01|P2034/);
      expect(rs.map((r) => r.status)).toEqual(['fulfilled', 'rejected']);
      expect(reasonOf(rs[1])).toContain('Bạn đã có yêu cầu thu kèm TR-');
      expect(await prisma.wasteRequest.count({ where: { userId: u.id, method: 'PICKUP' } })).toBe(1);
    });
    it(`same key in parallel (${label}) -> one row, both answers carry the same id, one event`, async () => {
      const { u, item, point } = await scene(free);
      const key = randKey();
      const lines = [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty: 3 }];
      const rs = await race(u, () => create(u, key, lines, 'DROP_OFF', point.id), () => create(u, key, lines, 'DROP_OFF', point.id));
      expect(errorsOf(rs).join()).not.toMatch(/40P01|P2034/);
      expect(errorsOf(rs)).toEqual([]);
      const ids = rs.map((r) => (r as PromiseFulfilledResult<{ id: string }>).value.id);
      expect(ids[0]).toBe(ids[1]);
      expect(await prisma.wasteRequest.count({ where: { userId: u.id } })).toBe(1);
      expect(await prisma.wasteEvent.count({ where: { requestId: ids[0], kind: 'REQUEST_CREATED' } })).toBe(1);
    });
  }
  it('MAIL_IN and PICKUP replays at max open ignore pointId and return the request just created, no new event', async () => {
    const { u, item, point } = await scene(free);
    await openFixture(u, item, point);
    const k1 = randKey();
    const k2 = randKey();
    const mail = await create(u, k1, [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty: 15 }], 'MAIL_IN', 'x1');
    const pick = await create(u, k2, [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty: 10 }], 'PICKUP', 'x1');
    expect(await prisma.wasteRequest.count({ where: { userId: u.id, status: 'OPEN' } })).toBe(3);
    await expect(create(u, k1, [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty: 15 }], 'MAIL_IN', 'x2')).resolves.toMatchObject({ id: mail.id });
    await expect(create(u, k2, [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty: 10 }], 'PICKUP', 'x2')).resolves.toMatchObject({ id: pick.id });
    expect(await prisma.wasteEvent.count({ where: { requestId: { in: [mail.id, pick.id] }, kind: 'REQUEST_CREATED' } })).toBe(2);
  });
  it('a different body under the same key is 409 and writes nothing', async () => {
    const { u, item, point } = await scene(free);
    const key = randKey();
    await create(u, key, [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty: 3 }], 'DROP_OFF', point.id);
    await expect(create(u, key, [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty: 4 }], 'DROP_OFF', point.id)).rejects.toThrow('Mã chống gửi lặp này đã dùng cho một yêu cầu khác');
    expect(await prisma.wasteRequest.count({ where: { userId: u.id } })).toBe(1);
  });
  it('DROP_OFF at a PARTNER whose OWNER lost the partner store is 409 (spec 17.3)', async () => {
    const { u, item, point } = await scene(free);
    await prisma.storefront.update({ where: { id: point.ownerStorefrontId! }, data: { type: 'CTV', isPartner: false } });
    await expect(create(u, randKey(), [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty: 3 }], 'DROP_OFF', point.id)).rejects.toThrow('Điểm nhận đang tạm ngừng, hãy chọn điểm khác');
    expect(await prisma.wasteRequest.count({ where: { userId: u.id } })).toBe(0);
  });
  it('a battery line (COMING_SOON hazardous item) is 409 and writes nothing', async () => {
    const { u, item, point } = await scene({ status: 'COMING_SOON', hazardous: true });
    await expect(create(u, randKey(), [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty: 1 }], 'DROP_OFF', point.id)).rejects.toThrow('Món này hiện chưa nhận, hãy bỏ khỏi danh sách');
    expect(await prisma.wasteRequest.count({ where: { userId: u.id } })).toBe(0);
  });
});
```

  `raceWithHold` fits every pair here: both contenders take `lockWasteCustomer` before the replay read, so the second queues on the held lock (registry 4.12 item 4). The fixture item is 1 point per unit (`createWasteItem` defaults `unitsPerPoint` 1, `pointsPerGroup` 1), so 15 units meet the MAIL_IN minimum of 15 and 10 units the PICKUP minimum of 10 (registry 4.2 defaults); two 40-point requests against the monthly cap of 60 leave 20.
```ts
function makeEdit(o: { row: unknown; hold: unknown; before: { itemId: string; declaredQty: number }[]; updateCount?: number }) {
  const order: string[] = [];
  const tx = {
    $executeRaw: jest.fn(async (..._sql: unknown[]) => { order.push('lock'); return 1; }),
    $queryRaw: jest.fn(async (..._sql: unknown[]) => { order.push('forUpdate'); return o.row ? [o.row] : []; }),
    wasteRequestLine: {
      findMany: jest.fn(async (_args: unknown) => { order.push('before'); return o.before; }),
      deleteMany: jest.fn(async (_args: unknown) => { order.push('deleteLines'); return { count: o.before.length }; }),
      createMany: jest.fn(async (_args: { data: Record<string, unknown>[] }) => { order.push('createLines'); return { count: 1 }; }),
    },
    wasteRequest: { updateMany: jest.fn(async (_args: { where: Record<string, unknown>; data: Record<string, unknown> }) => { order.push('update'); return { count: o.updateCount ?? 1 }; }) },
    wasteEvent: { create: jest.fn(async (_args: { data: Record<string, unknown> }) => { order.push('event'); return { id: 'e1' }; }) },
  };
  const prisma = { $transaction: jest.fn(async (fn: (t: typeof tx) => Promise<unknown>) => fn(tx)) };
  const hold = { validateAndHold: jest.fn(async (_tx: unknown, _input: Record<string, unknown>, _cfg: unknown) => { order.push('hold'); return o.hold; }) };
  const config = { snapshot: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(SNAP) };
  const svc = new WasteRequestsService(prisma as never, {} as never, hold as never, config as never, {} as never);
  const loadDetail = jest.spyOn(svc, 'loadDetail').mockResolvedValue({ id: 'r1' } as never);
  return { svc, tx, hold, loadDetail, order };
}

describe('WasteRequestsService.edit (spec 15.2 PATCH)', () => {
  const row = { id: 'r1', method: 'DROP_OFF', status: 'OPEN', orderId: null, trackingCode: null, pointId: 'p1', capDayKey: '2026-10-15', capMonthKey: '2026-10' };
  const HOLD = { pointId: 'p1', capDayKey: '2026-10-15', capMonthKey: '2026-10', expiresAt: null, lines: [{ itemId: 'carton', itemCode: 'CARTON', itemName: 'Thùng carton', unitLabel: 'thùng', unitsPerPoint: 2, pointsPerGroup: 1, dropsPerUnit: 1, gramsPerUnit: null, declaredQty: 8, hazardous: false }], estimatedPoints: 4, estimatedDrops: 8 };
  it('lock, FOR UPDATE on (id, userId), hold EDIT with stored keys, replace lines, conditional updateMany, one event', async () => {
    const { svc, tx, hold, order } = makeEdit({ row, hold: HOLD, before: [{ itemId: 'carton', declaredQty: 5 }] });
    await svc.edit('u1', 'r1', { lines: [{ itemId: 'carton', declaredQty: 8 }] });
    expect(order).toEqual(['lock', 'forUpdate', 'hold', 'before', 'deleteLines', 'createLines', 'update', 'event']);
    const [strings, ...values] = tx.$queryRaw.mock.calls[0] as [TemplateStringsArray, ...unknown[]];
    expect(strings.join('?')).toMatch(/FOR UPDATE/);
    expect(values).toEqual(['r1', 'u1']);
    expect(hold.validateAndHold.mock.calls[0]![1]).toMatchObject({ path: 'EDIT', method: 'DROP_OFF', pointId: 'p1', existing: { id: 'r1', pointId: 'p1', capDayKey: '2026-10-15', capMonthKey: '2026-10' } });
    expect(tx.wasteRequest.updateMany).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ where: { id: 'r1', userId: 'u1', status: 'OPEN', orderId: null, trackingCode: null }, data: { pointId: 'p1', estimatedPoints: 4, estimatedDrops: 8 } });
    expect(tx.wasteRequestLine.createMany.mock.calls[0]![0].data[0]).toMatchObject({ requestId: 'r1', itemId: 'carton', unitsPerPoint: 2, declaredQty: 8 });
    expect(tx.wasteRequestLine.createMany.mock.calls[0]![0].data[0]).not.toHaveProperty('hazardous');
    expect(tx.wasteEvent.create.mock.calls[0]![0].data).toMatchObject({ kind: 'REQUEST_EDITED', actorRole: 'CUSTOMER', actorUserId: 'u1', requestId: 'r1', meta: { before: { pointId: 'p1', lines: [{ itemId: 'carton', declaredQty: 5 }] }, after: { pointId: 'p1', lines: [{ itemId: 'carton', declaredQty: 8 }], estimatedPoints: 4 } } });
  });
  it('the update data never touches code, expiresAt, consentAt, capDayKey, capMonthKey, method', async () => {
    const { svc, tx } = makeEdit({ row, hold: HOLD, before: [] });
    await svc.edit('u1', 'r1', { lines: [{ itemId: 'carton', declaredQty: 8 }] });
    expect(Object.keys(tx.wasteRequest.updateMany.mock.calls[0]![0].data).sort()).toEqual(['estimatedDrops', 'estimatedPoints', 'pointId']);
  });
  it('DROP_OFF body pointId switches the point; MAIL_IN body pointId is ignored', async () => {
    const a = makeEdit({ row, hold: HOLD, before: [] });
    await a.svc.edit('u1', 'r1', { lines: [{ itemId: 'carton', declaredQty: 8 }], pointId: 'p2' });
    expect(a.hold.validateAndHold.mock.calls[0]![1].pointId).toBe('p2');
    const b = makeEdit({ row: { ...row, method: 'MAIL_IN', pointId: 'wh' }, hold: { ...HOLD, pointId: 'wh' }, before: [] });
    await b.svc.edit('u1', 'r1', { lines: [{ itemId: 'carton', declaredQty: 16 }], pointId: 'p2' });
    expect(b.hold.validateAndHold.mock.calls[0]![1].pointId).toBeNull();
  });
  it.each<[string, unknown, unknown]>([
    ['missing or foreign id', null, new NotFoundException('Không tìm thấy yêu cầu')],
    ['IN_TRANSIT', { ...row, status: 'IN_TRANSIT' }, new ConflictException('Yêu cầu này không còn sửa được')],
    ['attached PICKUP', { ...row, method: 'PICKUP', orderId: 'o1' }, new ConflictException('Yêu cầu này không còn sửa được')],
    ['tracking entered', { ...row, method: 'MAIL_IN', trackingCode: 'S1' }, new ConflictException('Yêu cầu này không còn sửa được')],
    ['CANCELLED', { ...row, status: 'CANCELLED' }, new ConflictException('Yêu cầu này không còn sửa được')],
    ['COMPLETED', { ...row, status: 'COMPLETED' }, new ConflictException('Yêu cầu này không còn sửa được')],
  ])('%s -> error, nothing written', async (_n, r, err) => {
    const { svc, tx, hold } = makeEdit({ row: r, hold: HOLD, before: [] });
    const call = svc.edit('u1', 'r1', { lines: [{ itemId: 'carton', declaredQty: 1 }] });
    await expect(call).rejects.toThrow(err as Error);
    await expect(call).rejects.toBeInstanceOf((err as Error).constructor);
    expect(hold.validateAndHold).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(tx.wasteRequest.updateMany).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(tx.wasteEvent.create).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
  it('updateMany hitting 0 rows is 409 and writes no event', async () => {
    const { svc, tx } = makeEdit({ row, hold: HOLD, before: [], updateCount: 0 });
    await expect(svc.edit('u1', 'r1', { lines: [{ itemId: 'carton', declaredQty: 8 }] })).rejects.toThrow('Yêu cầu này không còn sửa được');
    expect(tx.wasteEvent.create).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});
```

```ts
describe('waste edit races and edit rules (real Postgres, spec 17.3)', () => {
  const free = { dailyCapQty: null, monthlyCapQty: null };
  for (const [label, first] of [['A then B', 'A'], ['B then A', 'B']] as const) {
    const race = (u: { id: string }, a: () => Promise<unknown>, b: () => Promise<unknown>) =>
      first === 'A' ? raceWithHold(prisma, customerLock(u), a, b) : raceWithHold(prisma, customerLock(u), b, a);
    it(`edit x edit (${label}): both succeed in lock order, last writer's lines stay, two REQUEST_EDITED`, async () => {
      const { u, item, point } = await scene({ dailyCapQty: 20, monthlyCapQty: 60 });
      const r = await create(u, randKey(), [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty: 2 }], 'DROP_OFF', point.id);
      const a = () => svc.edit(u.id, r.id, { lines: [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty: 3 }] });
      const b = () => svc.edit(u.id, r.id, { lines: [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty: 4 }] });
      const rs = await race(u, a, b);
      expect(errorsOf(rs).join()).not.toMatch(/40P01|P2034/);
      expect(rs.every((x) => x.status === 'fulfilled')).toBe(true);
      const lines = await prisma.wasteRequestLine.findMany({ where: { requestId: r.id } });
      expect(lines.map((l) => l.declaredQty)).toEqual([first === 'A' ? 4 : 3]);
      expect(await prisma.wasteEvent.count({ where: { requestId: r.id, kind: 'REQUEST_EDITED' } })).toBe(2);
    });
    it(`edit x create at the month cap (${label}): the first holder wins, the other gets 409`, async () => {
      const { u, item, point } = await scene({ dailyCapQty: null, monthlyCapQty: 10 });
      const r = await create(u, randKey(), [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty: 4 }], 'DROP_OFF', point.id);
      const edit = () => svc.edit(u.id, r.id, { lines: [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty: 8 }] });
      const add = () => create(u, randKey(), [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty: 4 }], 'DROP_OFF', point.id);
      const rs = await race(u, edit, add);
      expect(errorsOf(rs).join()).not.toMatch(/40P01|P2034/);
      expect(rs.map((x) => x.status)).toEqual(['fulfilled', 'rejected']);
      expect(reasonOf(rs[1])).toContain(first === 'A' ? 'tháng này bạn chỉ còn gửi được 2' : 'tháng này bạn chỉ còn gửi được 6');
    });
  }
  it("customer B editing A's request gets 404 and the row is unchanged", async () => {
    const { u, item, point } = await scene(free);
    const other = await customer();
    const r = await create(u, randKey(), [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty: 2 }], 'DROP_OFF', point.id);
    const before = await prisma.wasteRequest.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: r.id }, include: { lines: true } });
    await expect(svc.edit(other.id, r.id, { lines: [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty: 3 }] })).rejects.toThrow('Không tìm thấy yêu cầu');
    expect(await prisma.wasteRequest.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: r.id }, include: { lines: true } })).toEqual(before);
  });
  it('a line whose item became HIDDEN is 409 on edit', async () => {
    const { u, item, point } = await scene(free);
    const r = await create(u, randKey(), [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty: 2 }], 'DROP_OFF', point.id);
    await prisma.wasteItem.update({ where: { id: item.id }, data: { status: 'HIDDEN' } });
    await expect(svc.edit(u.id, r.id, { lines: [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty: 3 }] })).rejects.toThrow('Món này hiện chưa nhận, hãy bỏ khỏi danh sách');
  });
  it('editing keeps a point that became unavailable; switching to an unavailable point is 409', async () => {
    const { u, owner, item, point } = await scene(free);
    const r = await create(u, randKey(), [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty: 2 }], 'DROP_OFF', point.id);
    await prisma.wastePoint.update({ where: { id: point.id }, data: { status: 'PAUSED' } });
    await expect(svc.edit(u.id, r.id, { lines: [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty: 3 }] })).resolves.toMatchObject({ id: r.id });
    const p2 = await createWastePoint(prisma, { kind: 'PARTNER', ownerUserId: owner.id, itemIds: [item.id] });
    pointIds.push(p2.id);
    await prisma.wastePoint.update({ where: { id: p2.id }, data: { status: 'PAUSED' } });
    await expect(svc.edit(u.id, r.id, { lines: [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty: 3 }], pointId: p2.id })).rejects.toThrow('Điểm nhận đang tạm ngừng, hãy chọn điểm khác');
  });
  it('MAIL_IN and PICKUP edit still succeeds with the lane flag off and at max open', async () => {
    const { u, item, wh } = await scene(free);
    const mail = await createWasteRequest(prisma, { userId: u.id, method: 'MAIL_IN', status: 'OPEN', pointId: wh.id, lines: [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty: 16 }] });
    const pick = await createWasteRequest(prisma, { userId: u.id, method: 'PICKUP', status: 'OPEN', pointId: wh.id, lines: [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty: 11 }] });
    const closedLanes = new WasteRequestsService(
      prisma,
      moduleRef.get(WasteAccessService),
      moduleRef.get(WasteHoldService),
      { snapshot: async () => ({ ...(await moduleRef.get(WasteConfigService).snapshot()), laneMailin: false, lanePickup: false, maxOpenRequests: 1 }) } as never,
      moduleRef.get(SystemConfigService),
    );
    await expect(closedLanes.edit(u.id, mail.id, { lines: [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty: 17 }] })).resolves.toMatchObject({ id: mail.id });
    await expect(closedLanes.edit(u.id, pick.id, { lines: [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty: 12 }] })).resolves.toMatchObject({ id: pick.id });
  });
  it('ship-locked customer: MAIL_IN edit is 403', async () => {
    const { u, item, wh } = await scene(free);
    const mail = await createWasteRequest(prisma, { userId: u.id, method: 'MAIL_IN', status: 'OPEN', pointId: wh.id, lines: [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty: 16 }] });
    await prisma.wasteUserFlags.create({ data: { userId: u.id, shipLockedAt: new Date() } });
    await expect(svc.edit(u.id, mail.id, { lines: [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty: 17 }] })).rejects.toThrow('Đường gửi kho và thu kèm đơn đang tạm dừng với tài khoản của bạn, liên hệ CSKH.');
  });
  it('edit keeps code, expiresAt, consentAt, capDayKey and capMonthKey', async () => {
    const { u, item, point } = await scene(free);
    const r = await create(u, randKey(), [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty: 2 }], 'DROP_OFF', point.id);
    const kept = { code: true, expiresAt: true, consentAt: true, capDayKey: true, capMonthKey: true } as const;
    const before = await prisma.wasteRequest.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: r.id }, select: kept });
    await svc.edit(u.id, r.id, { lines: [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty: 5 }] });
    expect(await prisma.wasteRequest.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: r.id }, select: kept })).toEqual(before);
  });
});
```

  `raceWithHold` fits both pairs: edit and create both take `lockWasteCustomer` first (registry 4.12 items 4 and 5). In the month-cap pair the loser's remaining quantity is 10 − 8 = 2 when the edit wins, and 10 − 4 = 6 when the create wins (the edit adds back its own stored share, spec 11.2). The MAIL_IN (16 units) and PICKUP (11 units) fixtures sit above the minimums of 15 and 10 (registry 4.2).
```ts
function makeTracking(o: { row: unknown; dup?: unknown }) {
  const order: string[] = [];
  const tx = {
    $executeRaw: jest.fn(async (...args: unknown[]) => {
      order.push((args[0] as TemplateStringsArray).join('?').includes('pg_advisory_xact_lock') ? 'lockTracking' : 'conflict');
      return 1;
    }),
    $queryRaw: jest.fn(async (..._sql: unknown[]) => { order.push('forUpdate'); return o.row ? [o.row] : []; }),
    wasteRequest: {
      findFirst: jest.fn(async (_args: unknown) => { order.push('ownDup'); return o.dup ?? null; }),
      updateMany: jest.fn(async (_args: { where: Record<string, unknown>; data: Record<string, unknown> }) => { order.push('update'); return { count: 1 }; }),
    },
    wasteEvent: { create: jest.fn(async (_args: { data: Record<string, unknown> }) => { order.push('event'); return { id: 'e1' }; }) },
  };
  const prisma = { $transaction: jest.fn(async (fn: (t: typeof tx) => Promise<unknown>) => fn(tx)) };
  const svc = new WasteRequestsService(prisma as never, {} as never, {} as never, { snapshot: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(SNAP) } as never, {} as never);
  const loadDetail = jest.spyOn(svc, 'loadDetail').mockResolvedValue({ id: 'r1' } as never);
  return { svc, tx, loadDetail, order };
}
function makeCancel(o: { row: unknown }) {
  const tx = {
    $queryRaw: jest.fn(async (..._sql: unknown[]) => (o.row ? [o.row] : [])),
    wasteRequest: { updateMany: jest.fn(async (_args: { where: Record<string, unknown>; data: Record<string, unknown> }) => ({ count: 1 })) },
    wasteEvent: { create: jest.fn(async (_args: { data: Record<string, unknown> }) => ({ id: 'e1' })) },
  };
  const prisma = { $transaction: jest.fn(async (fn: (t: typeof tx) => Promise<unknown>) => fn(tx)) };
  const svc = new WasteRequestsService(prisma as never, {} as never, {} as never, { snapshot: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(SNAP) } as never, {} as never);
  const loadDetail = jest.spyOn(svc, 'loadDetail').mockResolvedValue({ id: 'r1' } as never);
  return { svc, tx, loadDetail };
}

describe('WasteRequestsService.setTracking (spec 15.2)', () => {
  const open = { id: 'r1', method: 'MAIL_IN', status: 'OPEN', carrier: null, trackingCode: null, trackingNorm: null };
  it('TRACKING lock first, then own row FOR UPDATE, own-duplicate check, OPEN->IN_TRANSIT with inTransitAt and expiresAt null, conflict statement, TRACKING_SET', async () => {
    const { svc, tx, order } = makeTracking({ row: open });
    await svc.setTracking('u1', 'r1', { carrier: 'GHTK', trackingCode: ' s 123 abc ' });
    expect(order).toEqual(['lockTracking', 'forUpdate', 'ownDup', 'update', 'conflict', 'event']);
    const [lockStrings] = tx.$executeRaw.mock.calls[0] as [TemplateStringsArray, ...unknown[]];
    expect(lockStrings.join('?')).toContain('pg_advisory_xact_lock(731002, 0)');
    expect(tx.wasteRequest.findFirst).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ where: { userId: 'u1', id: { not: 'r1' }, trackingNorm: 'S123ABC', status: { in: ['OPEN', 'IN_TRANSIT', 'RECEIVED'] } }, select: { id: true } });
    const upd = tx.wasteRequest.updateMany.mock.calls[0]![0];
    expect(upd.where).toEqual({ id: 'r1', userId: 'u1', method: 'MAIL_IN', status: 'OPEN' });
    expect(upd.data).toMatchObject({ status: 'IN_TRANSIT', carrier: 'GHTK', trackingCode: 's 123 abc', trackingNorm: 'S123ABC', expiresAt: null, inTransitAt: expect.any(Date) });
    expect(tx.wasteEvent.create.mock.calls[0]![0].data).toMatchObject({ kind: 'TRACKING_SET', actorRole: 'CUSTOMER', actorUserId: 'u1', requestId: 'r1' });
  });
  it('IN_TRANSIT edit changes carrier and code only, TRACKING_EDITED; trackingConflict is never cleared', async () => {
    const { svc, tx } = makeTracking({ row: { ...open, status: 'IN_TRANSIT', carrier: 'GHTK', trackingCode: 'S123', trackingNorm: 'S123' } });
    await svc.setTracking('u1', 'r1', { carrier: 'GHN', trackingCode: 'S124' });
    const upd = tx.wasteRequest.updateMany.mock.calls[0]![0];
    expect(upd.where).toEqual({ id: 'r1', userId: 'u1', method: 'MAIL_IN', status: 'IN_TRANSIT' });
    expect(Object.keys(upd.data).sort()).toEqual(['carrier', 'trackingCode', 'trackingNorm']);
    expect(tx.wasteEvent.create.mock.calls[0]![0].data.kind).toBe('TRACKING_EDITED');
  });
  it('re-sending the same carrier and code on IN_TRANSIT is a no-op: no write, no event', async () => {
    const { svc, tx } = makeTracking({ row: { ...open, status: 'IN_TRANSIT', carrier: 'GHTK', trackingCode: 'S123', trackingNorm: 'S123' } });
    await svc.setTracking('u1', 'r1', { carrier: 'GHTK', trackingCode: 's123' });
    expect(tx.wasteRequest.updateMany).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(tx.wasteEvent.create).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
  it.each<[string, unknown, unknown, unknown]>([
    ['foreign or missing id', null, null, new NotFoundException('Không tìm thấy yêu cầu')],
    ['PICKUP', { ...open, method: 'PICKUP' }, null, new ConflictException('Yêu cầu này không dùng mã vận đơn')],
    ['DROP_OFF', { ...open, method: 'DROP_OFF' }, null, new ConflictException('Yêu cầu này không dùng mã vận đơn')],
    ['RECEIVED', { ...open, status: 'RECEIVED' }, null, new ConflictException('Yêu cầu này không còn nhập mã vận đơn được')],
    ['EXPIRED', { ...open, status: 'EXPIRED' }, null, new ConflictException('Yêu cầu này không còn nhập mã vận đơn được')],
    ['own duplicate', open, { id: 'r2' }, new ConflictException('Bạn đã dùng mã vận đơn này cho một yêu cầu khác')],
  ])('%s -> error, nothing written', async (_n, row, dup, err) => {
    const { svc, tx } = makeTracking({ row, dup });
    const call = svc.setTracking('u1', 'r1', { carrier: 'GHTK', trackingCode: 'S123' });
    await expect(call).rejects.toThrow(err as Error);
    await expect(call).rejects.toBeInstanceOf((err as Error).constructor);
    expect(tx.wasteRequest.updateMany).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(tx.wasteEvent.create).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});

describe('WasteRequestsService.cancel (spec 15.2)', () => {
  it('OPEN unattached: FOR UPDATE then conditional updateMany with closedAt, one CUSTOMER event', async () => {
    const { svc, tx } = makeCancel({ row: { id: 'r1', status: 'OPEN', orderId: null, closeReason: null } });
    await svc.cancel('u1', 'r1');
    const upd = tx.wasteRequest.updateMany.mock.calls[0]![0];
    expect(upd.where).toEqual({ id: 'r1', userId: 'u1', status: 'OPEN', orderId: null });
    expect(upd.data).toEqual({ status: 'CANCELLED', closedAt: expect.any(Date) });
    expect(tx.wasteEvent.create.mock.calls[0]![0].data).toMatchObject({ kind: 'REQUEST_CANCELLED', actorRole: 'CUSTOMER', actorUserId: 'u1', requestId: 'r1' });
  });
  it('already cancelled by the customer (closeReason null): replay, no write, no event', async () => {
    const { svc, tx, loadDetail } = makeCancel({ row: { id: 'r1', status: 'CANCELLED', orderId: null, closeReason: null } });
    await svc.cancel('u1', 'r1');
    expect(tx.wasteRequest.updateMany).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(tx.wasteEvent.create).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(loadDetail).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
  it.each<[string, unknown, unknown]>([
    ['foreign or missing id', null, new NotFoundException('Không tìm thấy yêu cầu')],
    ['cancelled by the ship lock', { id: 'r1', status: 'CANCELLED', orderId: null, closeReason: 'SHIP_LOCKED' }, new ConflictException('Yêu cầu này không còn huỷ được')],
    ['cancelled with its order', { id: 'r1', status: 'CANCELLED', orderId: 'o1', closeReason: 'ORDER_CANCELLED' }, new ConflictException('Yêu cầu này không còn huỷ được')],
    ['attached PICKUP', { id: 'r1', status: 'OPEN', orderId: 'o1', closeReason: null }, new ConflictException('Yêu cầu này không còn huỷ được')],
    ['IN_TRANSIT', { id: 'r1', status: 'IN_TRANSIT', orderId: null, closeReason: null }, new ConflictException('Yêu cầu này không còn huỷ được')],
  ])('%s -> error, nothing written', async (_n, row, err) => {
    const { svc, tx } = makeCancel({ row });
    const call = svc.cancel('u1', 'r1');
    await expect(call).rejects.toThrow(err as Error);
    await expect(call).rejects.toBeInstanceOf((err as Error).constructor);
    expect(tx.wasteRequest.updateMany).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});
```

```ts
describe('WasteCustomerController.setTracking and cancel (spec 15.1 OWN)', () => {
  const body = { carrier: 'GHTK', trackingCode: 'S1' };
  it('OWN gate: programme off and a waste-blocked customer still reach the service; guest and no phone are 403', async () => {
    const off = await build(false);
    await expect(off.c.setTracking('blocked', 'r1', body)).resolves.toEqual({ ok: 'setTracking' });
    await expect(off.c.cancel('blocked', 'r1')).resolves.toEqual({ ok: 'cancel' });
    expect(off.requests.setTracking).toHaveBeenCalledWith('blocked', 'r1', body);
    expect(off.requests.cancel).toHaveBeenCalledWith('blocked', 'r1');
    const on = await build(true);
    await expect(on.c.setTracking('guest', 'r1', body)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ForbiddenException);
    await expect(on.c.cancel('nophone', 'r1')).rejects.toThrow(new ForbiddenException('Bạn cần chia sẻ số điện thoại để dùng Đổi rác lấy quà.'));
    expect(on.requests.setTracking).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(on.requests.cancel).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
  it('PUT tracking and POST cancel with 10/min; cancel answers 200', () => {
    const p = WasteCustomerController.prototype;
    for (const m of [p.setTracking, p.cancel]) {
      expect(Reflect.getMetadata(`${THROTTLER_LIMIT}default`, m)).toBe(10);
      expect(Reflect.getMetadata(`${THROTTLER_TTL}default`, m)).toBe(60_000);
    }
    expect(Reflect.getMetadata(HTTP_CODE_METADATA, p.cancel)).toBe(200);
  });
});
```
