```ts
describe('waste tracking races (real Postgres, spec 17.3)', () => {
  let item: { id: string };
  let wh: { id: string };
  beforeAll(async () => {
    wh = await createWarehouse(prisma);
    item = await createWasteItem(prisma, { dailyCapQty: null, monthlyCapQty: null });
    itemIds.push(item.id);
  });
  async function mailIn(u: { id: string }, status: 'OPEN' | 'IN_TRANSIT' = 'OPEN', code?: string) {
    const r = await createWasteRequest(prisma, { userId: u.id, method: 'MAIL_IN', status, pointId: wh.id, lines: [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty: 16 }] });
    if (code !== undefined) {
      await prisma.wasteRequest.update({ where: { id: r.id }, data: { carrier: 'GHTK', trackingCode: code, trackingNorm: normalizeTracking(code), expiresAt: null, inTransitAt: new Date() } });
    }
    return r;
  }
  const conflictFlags = async (ids: string[]) =>
    (await prisma.wasteRequest.findMany({ where: { id: { in: ids } }, select: { trackingConflict: true } })).map((r) => r.trackingConflict);
  for (const [label, first] of [['A then B', 'A'], ['B then A', 'B']] as const) {
    const race = (a: () => Promise<unknown>, b: () => Promise<unknown>) =>
      first === 'A' ? raceWithHold(prisma, trackingLock, a, b) : raceWithHold(prisma, trackingLock, b, a);
    it(`two customers enter the same code (${label}): no deadlock, both flagged, neither told`, async () => {
      const [ua, ub] = [await customer(), await customer()];
      const [ra, rb] = [await mailIn(ua), await mailIn(ub)];
      const code = `IT${randCode(8)}`;
      const rs = await race(
        () => svc.setTracking(ua.id, ra.id, { carrier: 'GHTK', trackingCode: code }),
        () => svc.setTracking(ub.id, rb.id, { carrier: 'GHN', trackingCode: code.toLowerCase() }),
      );
      expect(errorsOf(rs).join()).not.toMatch(/40P01|P2034/);
      expect(rs.every((x) => x.status === 'fulfilled')).toBe(true);
      for (const x of rs) expect('trackingConflict' in (x as PromiseFulfilledResult<object>).value).toBe(false);
      const rows = await prisma.wasteRequest.findMany({ where: { id: { in: [ra.id, rb.id] } } });
      expect(rows.map((r) => [r.status, r.trackingConflict, r.expiresAt])).toEqual([['IN_TRANSIT', true, null], ['IN_TRANSIT', true, null]]);
    });
    it(`two customers swap codes (${label}): no deadlock, both flagged`, async () => {
      const [ua, ub] = [await customer(), await customer()];
      const [x, y] = [`IT${randCode(8)}`, `IT${randCode(8)}`];
      const [ra, rb] = [await mailIn(ua, 'IN_TRANSIT', x), await mailIn(ub, 'IN_TRANSIT', y)];
      const rs = await race(
        () => svc.setTracking(ua.id, ra.id, { carrier: 'GHTK', trackingCode: y }),
        () => svc.setTracking(ub.id, rb.id, { carrier: 'GHTK', trackingCode: x }),
      );
      expect(errorsOf(rs).join()).not.toMatch(/40P01|P2034/);
      expect(rs.every((r) => r.status === 'fulfilled')).toBe(true);
      expect(await conflictFlags([ra.id, rb.id])).toEqual([true, true]);
    });
    it(`re-tap of the same code races another customer's first entry (${label})`, async () => {
      const [ua, ub] = [await customer(), await customer()];
      const s = `IT${randCode(8)}`;
      const ra = await mailIn(ua, 'IN_TRANSIT', s);
      const rb = await mailIn(ub);
      const rs = await race(
        () => svc.setTracking(ua.id, ra.id, { carrier: 'GHTK', trackingCode: s }),
        () => svc.setTracking(ub.id, rb.id, { carrier: 'GHN', trackingCode: s }),
      );
      expect(errorsOf(rs).join()).not.toMatch(/40P01|P2034/);
      expect(rs.every((r) => r.status === 'fulfilled')).toBe(true);
      expect(await conflictFlags([ra.id, rb.id])).toEqual([true, true]);
      expect(await prisma.wasteEvent.count({ where: { requestId: ra.id, kind: 'TRACKING_EDITED' } })).toBe(0);
      expect(await prisma.wasteEvent.count({ where: { requestId: rb.id, kind: 'TRACKING_SET' } })).toBe(1);
    });
  }
  it('an OPEN MAIL_IN past expiresAt but not yet swept accepts the first code and clears expiresAt', async () => {
    const u = await customer();
    const r = await mailIn(u);
    await prisma.wasteRequest.update({ where: { id: r.id }, data: { expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() - 3_600_000) } });
    await expect(svc.setTracking(u.id, r.id, { carrier: 'GHTK', trackingCode: `IT${randCode(8)}` })).resolves.toMatchObject({ id: r.id });
    const row = await prisma.wasteRequest.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: r.id } });
    expect([row.status, row.expiresAt]).toEqual(['IN_TRANSIT', null]);
    expect(row.inTransitAt).toBeInstanceOf(Date);
  });
  it('own duplicate is caught after normalising spaces and case', async () => {
    const u = await customer();
    await mailIn(u, 'IN_TRANSIT', 'S 123');
    const r2 = await mailIn(u);
    await expect(svc.setTracking(u.id, r2.id, { carrier: 'GHTK', trackingCode: 's123 ' })).rejects.toThrow('Bạn đã dùng mã vận đơn này cho một yêu cầu khác');
    expect((await prisma.wasteRequest.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: r2.id } })).status).toBe('OPEN');
  });
  it('PICKUP and DROP_OFF reject a code and write nothing', async () => {
    const s = await scene({ dailyCapQty: null, monthlyCapQty: null });
    const pick = await createWasteRequest(prisma, { userId: s.u.id, method: 'PICKUP', status: 'OPEN', pointId: s.wh.id, lines: [{ itemId: s.item.id, declaredQty: 11 }] });
    const drop = await createWasteRequest(prisma, { userId: s.u.id, method: 'DROP_OFF', status: 'OPEN', pointId: s.point.id, lines: [{ itemId: s.item.id, declaredQty: 2 }] });
    for (const r of [pick, drop]) {
      const before = await prisma.wasteRequest.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: r.id } });
      await expect(svc.setTracking(s.u.id, r.id, { carrier: 'GHTK', trackingCode: 'S1' })).rejects.toThrow('Yêu cầu này không dùng mã vận đơn');
      expect((await prisma.wasteRequest.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: r.id } })).updatedAt).toEqual(before.updatedAt);
      expect(await prisma.wasteEvent.count({ where: { requestId: r.id } })).toBe(0);
    }
  });
  it('changing the code later keeps trackingConflict true', async () => {
    const [ua, ub] = [await customer(), await customer()];
    const s = `IT${randCode(8)}`;
    const ra = await mailIn(ua);
    await mailIn(ub, 'IN_TRANSIT', s);
    await svc.setTracking(ua.id, ra.id, { carrier: 'GHTK', trackingCode: s });
    await svc.setTracking(ua.id, ra.id, { carrier: 'GHTK', trackingCode: `IT${randCode(8)}` });
    expect((await prisma.wasteRequest.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: ra.id } })).trackingConflict).toBe(true);
  });
  it('a blocked customer can still enter a code and cancel', async () => {
    const u = await customer();
    const [r1, r2] = [await mailIn(u), await mailIn(u)];
    await prisma.wasteUserFlags.create({ data: { userId: u.id, blockedAt: new Date() } });
    await expect(svc.setTracking(u.id, r1.id, { carrier: 'GHTK', trackingCode: `IT${randCode(8)}` })).resolves.toMatchObject({ id: r1.id });
    await expect(svc.cancel(u.id, r2.id)).resolves.toMatchObject({ id: r2.id });
    expect((await prisma.wasteRequest.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: r2.id } })).status).toBe('CANCELLED');
  });
  it("customer B gets 404 on A's request for tracking and cancel; A's row unchanged", async () => {
    const [ua, ub] = [await customer(), await customer()];
    const r = await mailIn(ua);
    const before = await prisma.wasteRequest.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: r.id } });
    await expect(svc.setTracking(ub.id, r.id, { carrier: 'GHTK', trackingCode: 'S1' })).rejects.toThrow('Không tìm thấy yêu cầu');
    await expect(svc.cancel(ub.id, r.id)).rejects.toThrow('Không tìm thấy yêu cầu');
    expect((await prisma.wasteRequest.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: r.id } })).updatedAt).toEqual(before.updatedAt);
  });
  it('cancel twice: one CANCELLED row, one REQUEST_CANCELLED event, same DTO; cancel frees the cap', async () => {
    const { u, item: capped, point } = await scene({ dailyCapQty: null, monthlyCapQty: 10 });
    const r = await create(u, randKey(), [{ itemId: capped.id, declaredQty: 10 }], 'DROP_OFF', point.id);
    const one = await svc.cancel(u.id, r.id);
    const two = await svc.cancel(u.id, r.id);
    expect(two).toEqual(one);
    expect(await prisma.wasteEvent.count({ where: { requestId: r.id, kind: 'REQUEST_CANCELLED' } })).toBe(1);
    await expect(create(u, randKey(), [{ itemId: capped.id, declaredQty: 10 }], 'DROP_OFF', point.id)).resolves.toBeDefined();
    expect(await prisma.wasteRequest.count({ where: { userId: u.id, status: 'OPEN' } })).toBe(1);
  });
  it('cancel after a ship-lock system cancel is 409, not a replay', async () => {
    const u = await customer();
    const r = await mailIn(u);
    await prisma.wasteRequest.update({ where: { id: r.id }, data: { status: 'CANCELLED', closeReason: 'SHIP_LOCKED', closedAt: new Date() } });
    await expect(svc.cancel(u.id, r.id)).rejects.toThrow('Yêu cầu này không còn huỷ được');
  });
});
```

  `blockersFor(overrides)` is a local helper: `const { svc, tx } = build([]); Object.assign(tx, overrides); const err = await svc.process('req-1', 'admin-1').then(() => null, (e: unknown) => e);` and returns `{ blockers: err ? ((err as BadRequestException).getResponse() as { blockers: DeletionBlocker[] }).blockers : [], tx }` (the same entry point as the spec's `PARTNER_STORE_OPEN` test; the fake user is a DEALER with zero balances, so no other blocker appears). The describe has `afterEach(() => jest.restoreAllMocks())` because `build()` spies on `anonymize`. Change the spec's import line to `import { ACCOUNT_DELETION_BLOCKED, AccountDeletionService, type DeletionBlocker, type PartnerStoreBlockersPort } from './account-deletion.service';` (`DeletionBlocker` is exported at `account-deletion.service.ts:26`).

it('merge: waste rows move; duplicate keys renamed MERGED:<guest>:<key>; flags merged; point staff moved, deduped and promoted', async () => {
  const { guest, target } = await guestAndTarget({}, {});
  const wh = await createWarehouse(prisma);
  const item = await createWasteItem(prisma);
  const gReq = await createWasteRequest(prisma, { userId: guest.id, method: 'DROP_OFF', status: 'CANCELLED', pointId: wh.id, lines: [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty: 1 }] });
  const tReq = await createWasteRequest(prisma, { userId: target.id, method: 'DROP_OFF', status: 'CANCELLED', pointId: wh.id, lines: [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty: 1 }] });
  await prisma.wasteRequest.update({ where: { id: gReq.id }, data: { idempotencyKey: tReq.idempotencyKey } });
  const gRed = await createGiftRedemption(prisma, { userId: guest.id, status: 'FULFILLED' });
  const early = new Date('2026-09-01T00:00:00Z'); const late = new Date('2026-10-01T00:00:00Z');
  await prisma.wasteUserFlags.create({ data: { userId: guest.id, shipLockedAt: early, shipUnlockedAt: late } });
  await prisma.wasteUserFlags.create({ data: { userId: target.id, shipLockedAt: late, shipUnlockedAt: early, blockedAt: late } });
  const dupPoint = await createWastePoint(prisma, { kind: 'PARTNER' });
  const movePoint = await createWastePoint(prisma, { kind: 'PARTNER' });
  await prisma.wastePointStaff.create({ data: { pointId: dupPoint.id, userId: guest.id, role: 'OWNER', isActive: true } });
  await prisma.wastePointStaff.create({ data: { pointId: dupPoint.id, userId: target.id, role: 'STAFF', isActive: false } });
  await prisma.wastePointStaff.create({ data: { pointId: movePoint.id, userId: guest.id, role: 'STAFF', isActive: true } });
  zaloIdForNextCall = target.zaloId!;
  await auth.ensurePhoneForCurrentUser(guest.id, 'code', 'access');
  expect(await prisma.wasteRequest.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: gReq.id } })).toMatchObject({ userId: target.id, idempotencyKey: `MERGED:${guest.id}:${tReq.idempotencyKey}` });
  expect(await prisma.wasteRequest.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: tReq.id } })).toMatchObject({ userId: target.id, idempotencyKey: tReq.idempotencyKey });
  expect((await prisma.wasteGiftRedemption.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: gRed.id } })).userId).toBe(target.id);
  expect(await prisma.wasteUserFlags.findUnique({ where: { userId: guest.id } })).toBeNull();
  expect(await prisma.wasteUserFlags.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { userId: target.id } })).toMatchObject({ shipLockedAt: early, shipUnlockedAt: late, blockedAt: late });
  expect(await prisma.wastePointStaff.findMany({ where: { userId: { in: [guest.id, target.id] } }, orderBy: { pointId: 'asc' }, select: { pointId: true, userId: true, role: true, isActive: true } })).toEqual(
    [{ pointId: dupPoint.id, userId: target.id, role: 'OWNER', isActive: true }, { pointId: movePoint.id, userId: target.id, role: 'STAFF', isActive: true }].sort((a, b) => a.pointId.localeCompare(b.pointId)),
  );
  await cleanupWaste(prisma, { pointIds: [dupPoint.id, movePoint.id], itemIds: [item.id], giftIds: [gRed.giftId] });
});

The partner case never uses `expectBlocked`: that helper calls the outer `svc` (`moduleRef.get(AccountDeletionService)`, built without the optional `PARTNER_STORE_BLOCKERS` port), and without the port `collectBlockers` skips `ownerPartnerBlockers` (`account-deletion.service.ts:194`), so it builds its own service with the port as `d2-dealer-revoke-partner.race-spec.ts:94-99` does.

```ts
describe('waste blockers (spec 11.1, 14.4)', () => {
  it('an OPEN waste request blocks with WASTE_REQUEST_OPEN', async () => {
    const { user, request } = await customerWithRequest();
    const wh = await createWarehouse(prisma);
    const item = await createWasteItem(prisma);
    await createWasteRequest(prisma, { userId: user.id, method: 'DROP_OFF', status: 'OPEN', pointId: wh.id, lines: [{ itemId: item.id, declaredQty: 1 }] });
    const body = await expectBlocked(request.id, user.id, /yêu cầu Đổi rác chưa xong/);
    expect(body.blockers.map((b) => b.code)).toContain('WASTE_REQUEST_OPEN');
    await prisma.wasteRequest.deleteMany({ where: { userId: user.id } });
    await cleanupWaste(prisma, { itemIds: [item.id] });
  });
  it('a waste_user_flags row with shipLockedAt blocks with WASTE_UNDER_REVIEW', async () => {
    const { user, request } = await customerWithRequest();
    await prisma.wasteUserFlags.create({ data: { userId: user.id, shipLockedAt: new Date() } });
    const body = await expectBlocked(request.id, user.id, /Tài khoản đang được Tubu xem xét, liên hệ CSKH/);
    expect(body.blockers.map((b) => b.code)).toContain('WASTE_UNDER_REVIEW');
    await prisma.wasteUserFlags.delete({ where: { userId: user.id } });
  });
});

describe('waste partner point blocker (spec 11.1)', () => {
  let withPort: AccountDeletionService;
  beforeAll(() => {
    withPort = new AccountDeletionService(prisma, new MerchantFulfilmentService(prisma, {} as never, undefined, new SystemConfigService(prisma)));
  });
  it('owner of an ACTIVE PARTNER point is blocked; pausing the point removes the line', async () => {
    const { user, request } = await customerWithRequest();
    const point = await createWastePoint(prisma, { kind: 'PARTNER', ownerUserId: user.id });
    const run = () => withPort.process(request.id, admin.id).then(() => null, (e: unknown) => e);
    const err = await run();
    expect(err).toBeInstanceOf(BadRequestException);
    expect(((err as BadRequestException).getResponse() as { message: string }).message).toMatch(/điểm nhận Đổi rác đang hoạt động/);
    await prisma.wastePoint.update({ where: { id: point.id }, data: { status: 'PAUSED' } });
    const again = await run();
    if (again) expect(((again as BadRequestException).getResponse() as { message: string }).message).not.toMatch(/điểm nhận Đổi rác đang hoạt động/);
    await cleanupWaste(prisma, { pointIds: [point.id], storefrontIds: point.createdStorefront && point.ownerStorefrontId ? [point.ownerStorefrontId] : [] });
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement merge step 6b** in `auth.service.ts` `mergeGuestInto`, inside the existing tx after step 6 (all statements use `gid`, `tid` and `mergedPrefix` already defined there; the advisory lock and both `users` rows are already locked, registry 4.12 item 18). Each SQL statement below is its own call: `tx.$executeRaw` for every UPDATE, INSERT and DELETE (two renames in 1, the flags upsert and the flags delete in 2, the move and each promote in 3) and `tx.$queryRaw` for the `DELETE ... RETURNING` of 3; never two statements in one template (Prisma sends a parameterised template as one prepared statement, and Postgres rejects two commands there with 42601, which would abort every merging login):
  1. Rename duplicate keys set-based, then move (spec 11.1: a single `updateMany` hitting a unique key would break every later login):

```sql
UPDATE waste_requests g SET "idempotencyKey" = ${mergedPrefix}::text || g."idempotencyKey", "updatedAt" = now()
 WHERE g."userId" = ${gid} AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM waste_requests t WHERE t."userId" = ${tid} AND t."idempotencyKey" = g."idempotencyKey");
UPDATE waste_gift_redemptions g SET "idempotencyKey" = ${mergedPrefix}::text || g."idempotencyKey"
 WHERE g."userId" = ${gid} AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM waste_gift_redemptions t WHERE t."userId" = ${tid} AND t."idempotencyKey" = g."idempotencyKey");
```

     then `tx.wasteRequest.updateMany({ where: { userId: gid }, data: { userId: tid } })` and `tx.wasteGiftRedemption.updateMany({ where: { userId: gid }, data: { userId: tid } })`.
  2. Flags (earliest `shipLockedAt` and `blockedAt`, latest `shipUnlockedAt`; Postgres `LEAST`/`GREATEST` skip NULLs), then delete the guest row:

```sql
INSERT INTO waste_user_flags ("userId", "shipLockedAt", "shipUnlockedAt", "blockedAt", reason, "setByUserId", "updatedAt")
SELECT ${tid}, g."shipLockedAt", g."shipUnlockedAt", g."blockedAt", g.reason, g."setByUserId", now() FROM waste_user_flags g WHERE g."userId" = ${gid}
ON CONFLICT ("userId") DO UPDATE SET
  "shipLockedAt" = LEAST(waste_user_flags."shipLockedAt", EXCLUDED."shipLockedAt"),
  "blockedAt" = LEAST(waste_user_flags."blockedAt", EXCLUDED."blockedAt"),
  "shipUnlockedAt" = GREATEST(waste_user_flags."shipUnlockedAt", EXCLUDED."shipUnlockedAt"),
  reason = COALESCE(waste_user_flags.reason, EXCLUDED.reason),
  "setByUserId" = COALESCE(waste_user_flags."setByUserId", EXCLUDED."setByUserId"),
  "updatedAt" = now();
DELETE FROM waste_user_flags WHERE "userId" = ${gid};
```

  3. Point staff: move the guest rows whose point the target has no row at (`UPDATE waste_point_staff g SET "userId" = ${tid}, "updatedAt" = now() WHERE g."userId" = ${gid} AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM waste_point_staff t WHERE t."userId" = ${tid} AND t."pointId" = g."pointId")`); delete the rest with `tx.$queryRaw\`DELETE FROM waste_point_staff WHERE "userId" = ${gid} RETURNING "pointId", role::text AS role, "isActive"\``; for each returned row with `role = 'OWNER'` and `isActive`, after the delete: `UPDATE waste_point_staff SET role = 'OWNER', "isActive" = true, "updatedAt" = now() WHERE "userId" = ${tid} AND "pointId" = ${pointId}` (spec 11.1: promote after the delete so `waste_point_staff_one_owner_key` holds).

---
#### Review Focus candidates (M2)

1. A MAIL_IN customer who shipped on day 7 and types the waybill a few hours after `expiresAt`, before the daily sweep: the code is accepted, the request goes IN_TRANSIT and `expiresAt` becomes null (spec 12.3 last paragraph; a strict `expiresAt > now` condition would reject a parcel already on its way). Pinned by M2.9 (race case "an OPEN MAIL_IN past expiresAt but not yet swept accepts the first code and clears expiresAt").
2. The same waybill typed twice by one customer with different spacing or case (`S 123` vs `s123 `): the second request gets the own-duplicate 409, because the check runs on `trackingNorm`, not on the raw text. Pinned by M2.9 (race case "own duplicate is caught after normalising spaces and case").
3. A client bug that sends the same item twice in one body (`[{carton,2},{carton,3}]`): 400 `Mỗi món chỉ khai một dòng`, not a 500 from the `(requestId, itemId)` unique key and not a silent double count against the caps. Pinned by M2.4 (unit case "CREATE duplicate itemId lines").
4. A customer taps "Huỷ" on a PICKUP request that the ship lock already cancelled: 409 `Yêu cầu này không còn huỷ được`, not a replay that pretends the customer cancelled it (the replay only applies to `CANCELLED` with `closeReason` null). Pinned by M2.9 (unit case "cancelled by the ship lock" and race case "cancel after a ship-lock system cancel is 409, not a replay").
5. An admin reversal reason typed with a final full stop (`Gian lận số lượng.`): the customer notification reads one full stop, not two, because the template adds its own. Pinned by M2.2 (notifier cases "pointsReversed" and "stripTrailingStop").

#### Registry deviations

1. Splits (size limit of about 8 files and 12 steps per task). Registry section 3: "M2.1 WasteConfigService + public flags." is split into M2.1a (config service) and M2.1 (public flags). "M2.3 Access layer." is split into M2.3a (staff access, idempotency key, masking, messages) and M2.3 (`WasteAccessService`). For these two the last part keeps the bare registry id instead of a letter, because the plan index expands `M2-core` to M2.1 to M2.5 and M2.6a, the ids that the M3, M4 and M5 parts depend on. "M2.6 Customer reads + controller skeleton." is split into M2.6a (mapper and read service), M2.6b (catalogue and `/waste/me`) and M2.6c (controller). "M2.8 Create + edit." is split into M2.8a (create) and M2.8b (edit). In each split the first part inherits the registry dependencies and the last part carries the Produces of the registry id.
2. Registry 2.2: "C `customer/waste-requests.service.ts`; M controller (+create, edit); ... [M2.8]" and registry 2.9: "`customer/waste-requests.service.ts` | M2.8 → M2.9". M2.6a creates the file with the read methods (`list`, `detail`, `byOrder` are listed under `WasteRequestsService` in registry 4.7 and the read routes of M2.6 call them), so the edit order is M2.6a → M2.8a → M2.8b → M2.9. No other milestone touches the file.
3. Registry 1.2: "M2.3 ← M1.7." M2.3 also depends on M1.2, because its real-DB spec uses the M1.2 waste fixtures.
4. Registry 1.2: "M2.8 ← M2.6." M2.8a depends on M2.7, as the controller hot-spot order requires ("`customer/waste-customer.controller.ts` | M2.6 → M2.7 → M2.8 → M2.9") and because it reuses `WasteLineDto` from M2.7.
5. Registry 1.2: "M2.5 ← M2.2." M2.5 depends on M2.2 and M2.4, because it appends to `waste-core.race-spec.ts` after M2.3 and M2.4.
6. New files not in registry 2.2: `apps/api/src/modules/waste-exchange/core/waste-messages.ts` (+spec, M2.3a), shared Vietnamese sentences that are not pinned in registry 4.13 (registry 4.13: "Other caps/threshold violations: 409 with a full Vietnamese sentence chosen by M2.4"); `apps/api/test/integration-race/waste-core.race-spec.ts` (M2.3, appended by M2.4 and M2.5), the real-DB proof of the `availablePointIds`, `capUsage` and 15.6 SQL.
7. Registry 4.6 path matrix: "lane flags + maxOpen + consent + one unattached PICKUP ...: CREATE only". `WasteHoldInput` has no consent field, so the MAIL_IN packaging and postage check (registry 8.21) runs in `WasteRequestsService.create` right after the replay check and before `validateAndHold`. The order of checks in spec 11.2 is unchanged. The `consent: true` field itself is enforced by `WasteCreateDto` (`@Equals(true)`).
